# Analyzed Bolivian municipalities for Wikipedia editing support: extended census population import with age group parsing; built infobox, population pyramid, and council table generators; cached Wikidata P402 snapshots; automated matching of 64 municipalities to OSM via Overpass queries generating QuickStatements; and created 6 JOSM-ready `.osm` files for municipal boundaries absent from OSM, sourced from the humdata.org shapefile.

## User

Extend censo-2024-population.qmd with a new section that pulls tab "3" of POBLACION as population_age / popblacion_edad, follwing the categories laid out there. Don't analyze any of the data from the beginning, but rather parse based on our existing import scripts. The only differences is a long series of are and age categories rather than total/urban/rural in column G. Save the output in both English and Spanish under different filenames as done with autoidentificacion.

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_013Q8uy8fh2DF4rVks6Xb3TN"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 31.7KB, 782 lines, showing lines 1-782]\n     1→---\n     2→title: \"Bolivia 2024 Cens...[truncated, total 36994 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 31.7KB, 782 lines, showing lines 1-782]\n---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2024 Census: Population and Ethnic Identification by Geography\"\nformat: \n  html:\n    code-tools: true\n---\n\nThis script ingests the published data tables produced by the Bolivian Instituto Nacional de Estadística ([website](https://www.ine.gob.bo/)\\|Wikipedia) based on the 2024 Census, officially the Censo de Población y Vivienda 2024, which is [described in Spanish here](https://cpv2024.ine.gob.bo/index.php/censo-2024/). Data from the Census is published online in both tabulated and microdata (one-person, one-line, without names) format.\n\nIn this script, I'm working with the published, tabulated data created by INE rather than producing my own summaries of the microdata. The 18 tabulated datasets ([available here](https://cpv2024.ine.gob.bo/index.php/tabulados-sobre-la-tematica-pobreza/)) are organized by topic and included information for four geographic levels, as well as for rural and urban subsets within each of them. They are formatted into Excel sheets with 1398 lines of data for all of these levels, interleaved in a human readable format.\n\nThe advantage of using these documents in that one can cite them directly, rather than introducing my own (unpublished) data analysis into the chain of citation, adding an extra verification step to scholarly publications and to create a non-independent source if used on Wikipedia.\n\nBut I still want to be able to automate the process of reading the numbers off these sheets. That's what this script accomplishes.\n\nHere's the format that the script has to untangle:\n\n![](images/POBLACION-row-structure.png){fig-alt=\"A section of an Excel spreadsheet with eight headers and a dozen or so lines, with different levels of detail from \\\"País\\\" to \\\"Municipio/TIOC\tChuquisaca\tOropeza\tYotala\tUrbana\\\"\"}\n\n\n```{r setup}\n#| message: false\nlibrary(readxl)\nlibrary(tidyverse)\nlibrary(kableExtra)\n```\n\n## Part 1: Population by Geography (`POBLACION.xlsx`)\n\nSource: sheet \"1\" of `POBLACION.xlsx`. The header spans Excel rows 5–7; data begins at row 9. Excel column A is blank and is dropped automatically by `read_excel`, so 24 columns remain (B–Y).\n\n- **Cols 1–6** (B–G): row counter, geographic level, departamento, provincia, municipio, area\n- **Cols 7–15** (H–P): population counts for 2001 / 2012 / 2024 × (total, hombre, mujer)\n- **Cols 16–24** (Q–Y): derived percentages, same structure\n\nWithin each geographic level, rows come in triples: the entity total (area = entity name), then \"Urbana\", then \"Rural\".\n\n```{r load-population}\n#| message: false\nraw <- read_excel(\n  \"../bolivia-data/censo_2024/POBLACION.xlsx\",\n  sheet     = \"1\",\n  col_names = FALSE,\n  skip      = 8   # rows 1–8: title, three header rows, blank separator\n)\n\ncol_names <- c(\n  \"row_num\",\n  \"nivel\",\n  \"departamento\",\n  \"provincia\",\n  \"municipio\",\n  \"area\",\n  \"pop_2001_total\",  \"pop_2001_hombre\",  \"pop_2001_mujer\",\n  \"pop_2012_total\",  \"pop_2012_hombre\",  \"pop_2012_mujer\",\n  \"pop_2024_total\",  \"pop_2024_hombre\",  \"pop_2024_mujer\",\n  \"pct_2001_total\",  \"pct_2001_hombre\",  \"pct_2001_mujer\",\n  \"pct_2012_total\",  \"pct_2012_hombre\",  \"pct_2012_mujer\",\n  \"pct_2024_total\",  \"pct_2024_hombre\",  \"pct_2024_mujer\"\n)\n\nnames(raw) <- col_names\n\npop_raw <- raw |>\n  mutate(\n    row_num = as.integer(row_num),\n    across(starts_with(\"pop_\") | starts_with(\"pct_\"), as.numeric)\n  ) |>\n  filter(!is.na(row_num))\n```\n\n### Disaggregate by geographic level\n\nEach level has \"total\" rows (area = entity name) and subdivision rows (area = \"Urbana\" or \"Rural\").\n\n```{r disaggregate-population}\nsubdivisions <- c(\"Urbana\", \"Rural\")\n\npop_country        <- pop_raw |> filter(nivel == \"País\")\n\npop_dept           <- pop_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Departamento\", !area %in% subdivisions)\n\npop_dept_urb_rural <- pop_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Departamento\",  area %in% subdivisions)\n\npop_prov           <- pop_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Provincia\",     !area %in% subdivisions) |> \n  filter(provincia != \"Territorio Indígena Multiétnico\")\n\npop_prov_urb_rural <- pop_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Provincia\",      area %in% subdivisions) |> \n  filter(provincia != \"Territorio Indígena Multiétnico\")\n\npop_muni           <- pop_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\", !area %in% subdivisions)\n\npop_muni_urb_rural <- pop_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\",  area %in% subdivisions)\n```\n\n```{r expand-prov-lookup-table}\n#| eval: false\nprov_id_lookup_table <- readRDS(\"data/prov_id_lookup_table.rds\")\nprov_id_lookup_long <- prov_id_lookup_table |> \n  pivot_longer(cols = starts_with(\"province\"), names_to = \"prov_list\", values_to = \"province\") \nprov_id_lookup <- prov_id_lookup_long |>\n  distinct(id_prov, department, province)\n\npop_prov_with_id <- pop_prov |> left_join(prov_id_lookup, by = c(\"provincia\" = \"province\", \n                                             \"departamento\" = \"department\")) \npop_prov_with_id |> filter(is.na(id_prov)) |> select(departamento, provincia, id_prov) ->\n  pop_prov_missing_id\n\nprov_id_no_match <- prov_id_lookup |> \n  anti_join(pop_prov, by = c(\"province\" = \"provincia\", \n                             \"department\" = \"departamento\"))\n\n# Manual matches: pop_prov_missing_id → id_prov (same dept, similar name)\n# Beni / Territorio Indígena Multiétnico is a TIOC with no standard province code\nmanual_prov_match <- tribble(\n  ~provincia,                    ~departamento,  ~id_prov,\n  \"Sur Yungas\",                  \"La Paz\",       \"0211\",  # Sud Yungas\n  \"Sur Lípez\",                   \"Potosí\",       \"0510\",  # Sur Lipez / Sud Lípez\n  \"General Bernardino Bilbao\",   \"Potosí\",       \"0513\",  # General Bilbao\n  \"Avilez\",                      \"Tarija\",       \"0604\",  # Avilés\n  \"Valle Grande\",                \"Santa Cruz\",   \"0708\",  # Vallegrande\n  \"Obispo Santisteban\",          \"Santa Cruz\",   \"0710\",  # Obispo Santistéban\n  \"Ñuflo de Chávez\",             \"Santa Cruz\",   \"0711\",  # Ñuflo De Chávez\n  \"Ángel Sandoval\",              \"Santa Cruz\",   \"0712\"   # Angel Sandoval\n)\n\n# Build cpv2024 name → id_prov lookup from auto-matched + manual rows\ncpv_prov_name_lookup <- pop_prov_with_id |>\n  filter(!is.na(id_prov)) |>\n  select(id_prov, prov_cpv2024 = provincia) |>\n  bind_rows(manual_prov_match |> select(id_prov, prov_cpv2024 = provincia)) |>\n  distinct(id_prov, .keep_all = TRUE)\n\n# Add prov_cpv2024 column to prov_id_lookup_table\nprov_id_lookup_table <- readRDS(\"data/prov_id_lookup_table.rds\") # reload for clean merge\nprov_id_lookup_table <- prov_id_lookup_table |>\n  left_join(cpv_prov_name_lookup, by = \"id_prov\") |> \n  relocate(prov_cpv2024, .before=department) |> \n  rename(province_cpv2024 = prov_cpv2024) |>\n  # recount distinct province names per department to identify duplicates\n  rowwise() |> mutate(\n    n_unique = n_distinct(c(province_gb2014, province_anexo, province_census, \n                            province_gadm, province_cpv2024), na.rm = TRUE)\n  ) |> \n  ungroup()\n\n# saveRDS(prov_id_lookup_table, \"data/prov_id_lookup_table.rds\")\n# \n# source(\"src/update-versioned-archive.R\")\n# update_versioned_archive(prov_id_lookup_table, \"../ultimate-consequences/data/prov_id_lookup_table.rds\")\n\n```\n\n```{r add-ine-code}\nmuni_id_lookup_table <- readRDS(\"data/muni_id_lookup_table.rds\")\ncrosswalk_muni <- muni_id_lookup_table |>\n  select(muni_cpv2024, department, id_muni) |>\n  rename(ine_code = id_muni)\n\nprov_id_lookup_table <- readRDS(\"data/prov_id_lookup_table.rds\")\ncrosswalk_prov <- prov_id_lookup_table |>\n  select(province_cpv2024, department, id_prov) |>\n  rename(ine_code = id_prov)\n\ndep_crosswalk <- crosswalk_prov |>\n  mutate(id_dep = str_extract(ine_code, \"^\\\\d{2}\")) |>\n  select(-province_cpv2024, -ine_code) |> \n  distinct()\n\nadd_ine_code_muni <- function(df) {\n  df |>\n    left_join(crosswalk_muni, by = c(\"municipio\" = \"muni_cpv2024\", \"departamento\" = \"department\")) |>\n    relocate(ine_code, municipio, provincia, departamento, area, nivel)\n}\n\nadd_ine_code_prov <- function(df) {\n  df |>\n    left_join(crosswalk_prov, by = c(\"provincia\" = \"province_cpv2024\", \"departamento\" = \"department\")) |>\n    relocate(ine_code, provincia, departamento, area, nivel) |>\n    select(-municipio)\n}\n\nadd_ine_code_dept <- function(df) {\n  df |>\n    left_join(dep_crosswalk, by = c(\"departamento\" = \"department\")) |>\n    relocate(id_dep, departamento, area, nivel) |>\n    select(-provincia, -municipio)\n}\n\npop_muni           <- add_ine_code_muni(pop_muni)\npop_muni_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_muni(pop_muni_urb_rural)\npop_prov           <- add_ine_code_prov(pop_prov)\npop_prov_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_prov(pop_prov_urb_rural)\npop_dept           <- add_ine_code_dept(pop_dept)\npop_dept_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_dept(pop_dept_urb_rural)\n```\n\n### Quick checks\n\n```{r checks-population}\n#| echo: false\n#| asis: true\ncat(\"Country rows:           \", nrow(pop_country),       \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept totals:            \", nrow(pop_dept),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept urban+rural rows:  \", nrow(pop_dept_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province totals:        \", nrow(pop_prov),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province urban+rural:   \", nrow(pop_prov_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality totals:    \", nrow(pop_muni),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality urb+rural: \", nrow(pop_muni_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\n```\n\n```{r preview-dept}\npop_dept |>\n  select(departamento, pop_2024_total, pop_2024_hombre, pop_2024_mujer) |>\n  kbl() |> kable_classic()\n```\n\n```{r preview-muni}\npop_muni |>\n  select(departamento, provincia, municipio, pop_2024_total, pop_2024_hombre, pop_2024_mujer) |>\n  head(12) |>\n  kbl() |> kable_classic()\n```\n\n### Save population outputs\n\n```{r prep-for-saving}\n#| echo: true\n\n# These have all Spanish names except for \"pop\" which should be \"pob\"\nes_var_name_fix <- function(x) {\n  x |> rename_with(~ stringr::str_replace(., \"^pop\", \"pob\"), starts_with(\"pop\"))\n}\n\npop_raw_es <- es_var_name_fix(pop_raw)\npop_country_es <- es_var_name_fix(pop_country)\npop_dept_es <- es_var_name_fix(pop_dept)\npop_dept_urb_rural_es <- es_var_name_fix(pop_dept_urb_rural)\npop_prov_es <- es_var_name_fix(pop_prov)\npop_prov_urb_rural_es <- es_var_name_fix(pop_prov_urb_rural)\npop_muni_es <- es_var_name_fix(pop_muni)\npop_muni_urb_rural_es <- es_var_name_fix(pop_muni_urb_rural)\n\nenglish_var_name_fix <- function(x) {\n  x |>\n    rename(\n      department = any_of(\"departamento\"),\n      province = any_of(\"provincia\"),\n      municipality = any_of(\"municipio\"),\n      level = any_of(\"nivel\")\n    ) |>\n    rename_with(~ stringr::str_replace(., \"_mujer\", \"_female\"), ends_with(\"mujer\")) |>\n    rename_with(~ stringr::str_replace(., \"_hombre\", \"_male\"), ends_with(\"hombre\"))\n}\n\npop_raw_en <- english_var_name_fix(pop_raw)\npop_country_en <- english_var_name_fix(pop_country)\npop_dept_en <- english_var_name_fix(pop_dept)\npop_dept_urb_rural_en <- english_var_name_fix(pop_dept_urb_rural)\npop_prov_en <- english_var_name_fix(pop_prov)\npop_prov_urb_rural_en <- english_var_name_fix(pop_prov_urb_rural)\npop_muni_en <- english_var_name_fix(pop_muni)\npop_muni_urb_rural_en <- english_var_name_fix(pop_muni_urb_rural)\n\n```\n\n```{r checks-output}\n#| echo: false\n#| asis: true\ncat(\"English output datasets:\\n\")\ncat(\"Country rows:           \", nrow(pop_country_en),        \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept totals:            \", nrow(pop_dept_en),            \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept urban+rural rows:  \", nrow(pop_dept_urb_rural_en),  \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province totals:        \", nrow(pop_prov_en),            \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province urban+rural:   \", nrow(pop_prov_urb_rural_en),  \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality totals:    \", nrow(pop_muni_en),            \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality urb+rural: \", nrow(pop_muni_urb_rural_en),  \"\\n\")\n\ncat(\"\\nSpanish output datasets:\\n\")\ncat(\"Country rows:           \", nrow(pop_country_es),        \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept totals:            \", nrow(pop_dept_es),            \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept urban+rural rows:  \", nrow(pop_dept_urb_rural_es),  \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province totals:        \", nrow(pop_prov_es),            \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province urban+rural:   \", nrow(pop_prov_urb_rural_es),  \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality totals:    \", nrow(pop_muni_es),            \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality urb+rural: \", nrow(pop_muni_urb_rural_es),  \"\\n\")\n```\n\n```{r save-population-data }\n#| eval: false\n#| echo: true\n\n# This code will not be run, since the data is already saved.\nsaveRDS(pop_raw_es,           \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_raw.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_country_es,       \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_pais.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_dept_es,          \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_departamento.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_dept_urb_rural_es,\"data/cpv2024/poblacion_departamento_urb_rural.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_prov_es,          \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_provincia.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_prov_urb_rural_es,\"data/cpv2024/poblacion_provincia_urb_rural.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_muni_es,          \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_municipio.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_muni_urb_rural_es,\"data/cpv2024/poblacion_municipio_urb_rural.rds\")\n\n# This code will not be run, since the data is already saved.\nsaveRDS(pop_raw_en,           \"data/cpv2024/population_raw.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_country_en,       \"data/cpv2024/population_country.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_dept_en,          \"data/cpv2024/population_department.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_dept_urb_rural_en,\"data/cpv2024/population_department_urb_rural.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_prov_en,          \"data/cpv2024/population_province.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_prov_urb_rural_en,\"data/cpv2024/population_province_urb_rural.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_muni_en,          \"data/cpv2024/population_municipality.rds\")\nsaveRDS(pop_muni_urb_rural_en,\"data/cpv2024/population_municipality_urb_rural.rds\")\n\n```\n\n------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n## Part 2: Ethnic Self-Identification (`AUTOIDENTIFICACION.xlsx`, sheet 3)\n\nSource: sheet \"3\" of `AUTOIDENTIFICACION.xlsx`. This table reports, for each geographic unit and urban/rural subdivision, the number of people who declared belonging to each of 133 indigenous nations, pueblos, and ethnic categories in the 2024 census. The `total` column is the count of all declarations (a person who identified with multiple groups is counted once per declaration).\n\nThe header spans two rows (Excel rows 5–6):\n\n- **Row 5** geographic keys + `TOTAL` + merged header \"DECLARACIONES DE AUTOIDENTIFICACIÓN CENSO 2024\" spanning all ethnic-group columns.\n- **Row 6** individual ethnic-group names (133 names, cols 7–139).\n\nData begins at row 7. Footnote rows at the bottom of the sheet (with long `nivel` strings) are removed during cleaning.\n\n```{r load-autoidentificacion}\n#| message: false\nauto_path <- \"../bolivia-data/censo_2024/AUTOIDENTIFICACION.xlsx\"\n\n# Row 6 of the sheet holds the 133 ethnic-group names; those become the trailing\n# column names. Read it separately before reading the data.\nethnic_group_names <- read_excel(\n  auto_path,\n  sheet     = \"3\",\n  col_names = FALSE,\n  skip      = 5,   # skip rows 1–5; row 6 is the one we want\n  n_max     = 1\n) |>\n  unlist(use.names = FALSE)\n\n# Assemble the full column-name vector: 6 geographic keys + 133 ethnic groups.\nauto_col_names <- c(\n  \"nivel\", \"departamento\", \"provincia\", \"municipio\", \"area\", \"total\",\n  ethnic_group_names\n)\n\n# Read data rows (starting at Excel row 7).\nauto_raw <- read_excel(\n  auto_path,\n  sheet     = \"3\",\n  col_names = FALSE,\n  skip      = 6\n)\nnames(auto_raw) <- auto_col_names\n\nauto_raw <- auto_raw |>\n  mutate(across(c(total, all_of(ethnic_group_names)), as.numeric)) |>\n  # Drop footnote rows: they have very long nivel strings, not short level names\n  filter(!is.na(nivel), nchar(nivel) <= 20)\n```\n\n### Disaggregate by geographic level\n\nThe row structure mirrors Part 1: each geographic entity appears three times (total, Urbana, Rural). The `nivel` labels used here are identical to those in `POBLACION.xlsx`.\n\n```{r disaggregate-autoidentificacion}\nauto_country        <- auto_raw |> filter(nivel == \"País\",            !area %in% subdivisions)\n\nauto_dept           <- auto_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Departamento\", !area %in% subdivisions)\n\nauto_dept_urb_rural <- auto_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Departamento\",  area %in% subdivisions)\n\nauto_prov           <- auto_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Provincia\",     !area %in% subdivisions)\n\nauto_prov_urb_rural <- auto_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Provincia\",      area %in% subdivisions)\n\nauto_muni           <- auto_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\", !area %in% subdivisions)\n\nauto_muni_urb_rural <- auto_raw |>\n  filter(nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\",  area %in% subdivisions)\n```\n\n```{r add-ine-code-autoidentificacion}\nauto_muni           <- add_ine_code_muni(auto_muni)\nauto_muni_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_muni(auto_muni_urb_rural)\nauto_prov           <- add_ine_code_prov(auto_prov)\nauto_prov_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_prov(auto_prov_urb_rural)\nauto_dept           <- add_ine_code_dept(auto_dept)\nauto_dept_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_dept(auto_dept_urb_rural)\n```\n\n### Quick checks\n\n```{r checks-autoidentificacion}\n#| echo: false\n#| asis: true\ncat(\"Country rows:           \", nrow(auto_country),       \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept totals:            \", nrow(auto_dept),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept urban+rural rows:  \", nrow(auto_dept_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province totals:        \", nrow(auto_prov),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province urban+rural:   \", nrow(auto_prov_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality totals:    \", nrow(auto_muni),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality urb+rural: \", nrow(auto_muni_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\n```\n\n```{r preview-auto-dept}\n# Total declarations by department (top 5 groups)\ntop5 <- auto_muni |>\n  select(all_of(ethnic_group_names)) |>\n  summarise(across(everything(), sum, na.rm = TRUE)) |>\n  pivot_longer(everything(), names_to = \"grupo\", values_to = \"n\") |>\n  slice_max(n, n = 5)\n\nauto_dept |>\n  select(departamento, total, Quechua, Aymara, Chiquitano, Guaraní, Mojeño) |>\n  arrange(desc(total)) |>\n  kbl() |> kable_classic()\n```\n\n```{r preview-auto-muni}\nauto_muni |>\n  select(departamento, provincia, municipio, total, Quechua, Aymara) |>\n  arrange(desc(total)) |> \n  head(12) |>\n  kbl() |> kable_classic()\n```\n\n### Save ethnic identification outputs\n\n```{r save-autoidentificacion}\n#| eval: false\n#| echo: true\n# This code will not be run, since the data is already saved.\nsaveRDS(auto_raw,           \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_raw.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_country,       \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_pais.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_dept,          \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_departamento.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_dept_urb_rural,\"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_departamento_urb_rural.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_prov,          \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_provincia.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_prov_urb_rural,\"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_provincia_urb_rural.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_muni,          \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_municipio.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_muni_urb_rural,\"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_municipio_urb_rural.rds\")\n\nauto_raw_en <- english_var_name_fix(auto_raw)\nauto_country_en <- english_var_name_fix(auto_country)\nauto_dept_en <- english_var_name_fix(auto_dept)\nauto_dept_urb_rural_en <- english_var_name_fix(auto_dept_urb_rural)\nauto_prov_en <- english_var_name_fix(auto_prov)\nauto_prov_urb_rural_en <- english_var_name_fix(auto_prov_urb_rural)\nauto_muni_en <- english_var_name_fix(auto_muni)\nauto_muni_urb_rural_en <- english_var_name_fix(auto_muni_urb_rural)\n\n# This code will not be run, since the data is already saved.\nsaveRDS(auto_raw_en,           \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_raw.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_country_en,       \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_country.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_dept_en,          \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_department.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_dept_urb_rural_en,\"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_department_urb_rural.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_prov_en,          \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_province.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_prov_urb_rural_en,\"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_province_urb_rural.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_muni_en,          \"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_municipality.rds\")\nsaveRDS(auto_muni_urb_rural_en,\"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_municipality_urb_rural.rds\")\n```\n\n------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n## Part 3: Language Use and Functionalizing the Process\n\n### Helper functions\n\n```{r part3-helpers}\n# ── Constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n# Group suffix map: cleaned group label → suffix appended to language name.\n# Empty string = no suffix (Idiomas oficiales is the default group).\ndefault_group_suffix_map <- c(\n  \"idiomas_oficiales\"   = \"\",\n  \"idioma_oficiales\"    = \"\",    # typo present in 2024 section of IDIOMAS_2\n  \"idiomas_extranjeros\" = \"ext\"\n)\n\n# Subdivision values keyed by the last_var column name.\nlast_var_subdivisions <- list(\n  area = c(\"Urbana\", \"Rural\"),\n  sexo = c(\"Hombres\", \"Mujeres\")\n)\n\n# ── clean_label ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Normalise a string for use as a column name segment:\n# strip footnote markers (e.g. \"(2)\") → lowercase → strip accents →\n# non-alphanumeric runs → \"_\" → trim edge \"_\"\nclean_label <- function(x) {\n  x |>\n    (\\(s) gsub(\"\\\\(\\\\d+\\\\)\", \"\", s))() |>   # remove footnote markers like (2), (4)\n    tolower() |>\n    stringi::stri_trans_general(\"Latin-ASCII\") |>\n    (\\(s) gsub(\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"_\", s))() |>\n    (\\(s) gsub(\"^_+|_+$\", \"\", s))()\n}\n\n# ── parse_tab_headers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Reads the multi-row header block and returns a character vector of column\n# names, constructed bottom-to-top:\n#   individual language name → group suffix → year\n#\n# For aggregate columns (no language name in the deepest row), the cleaned\n# group label is used as the base name instead.\n#\n# Parameters:\n#   header_skip      Excel rows to skip before the year-label row.\n#                    Default 4: row 5 = year, row 6 = group, row 7 = lang.\n#   n_geo_cols       Fixed geo columns at the left (default 5).\n#   last_var         Name for the 5th geo column (default \"area\").\n#   group_suffix_map Named character vector: clean group label → suffix string.\nparse_tab_headers <- function(\n    path,\n    sheet,\n    n_geo_cols       = 5,\n    header_skip      = 4,\n    last_var         = \"area\",\n    geo_col_names    = NULL,      # override default geo column names if needed\n    group_suffix_map = default_group_suffix_map\n) {\n  if (is.null(geo_col_names))\n    geo_col_names <- c(\"nivel\", \"departamento\", \"provincia\", \"municipio\", last_var)\n  stopifnot(length(geo_col_names) == n_geo_cols)\n\n  h <- read_excel(path, sheet = sheet, col_names = FALSE,\n                  skip = header_skip, n_max = 3)\n\n  data_names <- h |>\n    mutate(hrow = row_number()) |>\n    pivot_longer(-hrow, names_to = \"col\", values_to = \"val\") |>\n    mutate(col_pos = as.integer(str_extract(col, \"\\\\d+\"))) |>\n    filter(col_pos > n_geo_cols) |>\n    select(hrow, col_pos, val) |>\n    pivot_wider(names_from = hrow, values_from = val, names_prefix = \"row\") |>\n    arrange(col_pos) |>\n    # row1 = year, row2 = group — both use merged cells, so fill forward NAs\n    fill(row1, row2, .direction = \"down\") |>\n    mutate(\n      year_c  = clean_label(row1),\n      group_c = clean_label(row2),\n      lang_c  = if_else(!is.na(row3), clean_label(row3), NA_character_),\n      suffix  = unname(group_suffix_map[group_c]),\n      col_name = case_when(\n        # Aggregate column: the group itself is the variable\n        is.na(lang_c)            ~ paste(group_c, year_c, sep = \"_\"),\n        # Regular column, default group (no suffix or unknown group)\n        is.na(suffix) | suffix == \"\" ~ paste(lang_c, year_c, sep = \"_\"),\n        # Regular column with an explicit suffix (e.g. \"ext\")\n        TRUE                     ~ paste(lang_c, suffix, year_c, sep = \"_\")\n      )\n    ) |>\n    pull(col_name)\n\n  c(geo_col_names, data_names)\n}\n\n# ── read_tab_raw ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Loads one Excel sheet using parse_tab_headers() for column names, then\n# performs standard row-level cleaning (drop NA nivel, drop footnote rows).\n#\n# data_skip: Excel rows to skip before data begins (default 7 for IDIOMAS_2).\nread_tab_raw <- function(\n    path,\n    sheet,\n    stem,\n    n_geo_cols       = 5,\n    header_skip      = 4,\n    data_skip        = 7,\n    last_var         = \"area\",\n    group_suffix_map = default_group_suffix_map\n) {\n  col_names <- parse_tab_headers(\n    path, sheet,\n    n_geo_cols       = n_geo_cols,\n    header_skip      = header_skip,\n    last_var         = last_var,\n    group_suffix_map = group_suffix_map\n  )\n\n  df <- read_excel(path, sheet = sheet, col_names = FALSE, skip = data_skip)\n  names(df) <- col_names\n\n  df |>\n    mutate(across(-all_of(col_names[seq_len(n_geo_cols)]), as.numeric)) |>\n    filter(!is.na(nivel), nchar(nivel) <= 20)\n}\n\n# ── disaggregate_tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Splits a raw frame into a named list of geographic slices.\n#\n# last_var controls both the subdivision column name and which values count as\n# subdivisions (looked up from last_var_subdivisions):\n#   \"area\" (default) → c(\"Urbana\", \"Rural\"),  keys: *_urb_rural\n#   \"sexo\"           → c(\"Hombres\", \"Mujeres\"), keys: *_sexo\ndisaggregate_tab <- function(raw_df, last_var = \"area\") {\n  subdivisions <- last_var_subdivisions[[last_var]]\n  if (is.null(subdivisions)) {\n    stop(\"Unknown last_var '\", last_var, \"'. Known: \",\n         paste(names(last_var_subdivisions), collapse = \", \"))\n  }\n  sub_suffix <- switch(last_var, area = \"urb_rural\", sexo = \"sexo\", last_var)\n\n  is_sub  <- function(df) df[[last_var]] %in% subdivisions\n  drop_tim <- function(df) filter(df, provincia != \"Territorio Indígena Multiétnico\")\n\n  level_map <- c(dept = \"Departamento\", prov = \"Provincia\", muni = \"Municipio/TIOC\")\n\n  lst <- list(\n    raw     = raw_df,\n    country = filter(raw_df, nivel == \"País\")\n  )\n\n  for (key in names(level_map)) {\n    base <- filter(raw_df, nivel == level_map[[key]])\n    if (key == \"prov\") base <- drop_tim(base)\n    lst[[key]]                               <- filter(base, !is_sub(base))\n    lst[[paste(key, sub_suffix, sep = \"_\")]] <- filter(base,  is_sub(base))\n  }\n  lst\n}\n\n# ── attach_ine_codes ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Applies add_ine_code_dept/prov/muni() to matching list slots.\n# last_var: if not \"area\", temporarily renames the column to \"area\" before\n# calling the helpers (which hardcode that name) and renames it back after.\nattach_ine_codes <- function(lst, last_var = \"area\") {\n  needs_rename <- last_var != \"area\"\n  prep   <- function(df) if (needs_rename) rename(df, area = all_of(last_var)) else df\n  unprep <- function(df) if (needs_rename) rename(df, !!last_var := area) else df\n\n  for (key in names(lst)) {\n    if (!is.data.frame(lst[[key]])) next\n    if (startsWith(key, \"dept\")) lst[[key]] <- unprep(add_ine_code_dept(prep(lst[[key]])))\n    if (startsWith(key, \"prov\")) lst[[key]] <- unprep(add_ine_code_prov(prep(lst[[key]])))\n    if (startsWith(key, \"muni\")) lst[[key]] <- unprep(add_ine_code_muni(prep(lst[[key]])))\n  }\n  lst\n}\n\n# ── save_tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Saves each data-frame slot as {dir}/{stem}_{key}.rds.\n#\n# save_last_var_only: if TRUE, only save slots whose key contains last_var\n# (e.g. \"dept_sexo\", \"prov_sexo\", \"muni_sexo\"). Use this when a second\n# breakdown (sex) shares a stem with an existing breakdown (area) to avoid\n# overwriting the totals and the other subdivision's files.\nsave_tab <- function(lst, stem, dir = \"data/cpv2024/\",\n                     last_var = NULL, save_last_var_only = FALSE) {\n  for (key in names(lst)) {\n    if (!is.data.frame(lst[[key]])) next\n    if (save_last_var_only && !is.null(last_var) && !grepl(last_var, key)) next\n    saveRDS(lst[[key]], file.path(dir, paste0(stem, \"_\", key, \".rds\")))\n  }\n  invisible(lst)\n}\n```\n\n### Language declared spoken (`IDIOMAS_2.xlsx`, sheet \"8\")\n\nSource: sheet \"8\" of `IDIOMAS_2.xlsx`. This table counts, for each geographic unit and urban/rural subdivision, how many people declared speaking each language in 2012 and 2024. The header spans three rows (Excel rows 5–7): year block (2012 / 2024), group within year (Idiomas oficiales / Idiomas extranjeros / aggregate subtotals), and individual language name.\n\n```{r load-idiomas-hablados}\n#| message: false\npath_idiomas2 <- \"../bolivia-data/censo_2024_reportes/tabulados_descarga_completa/IDIOMAS_2.xlsx\"\n\nidiomas_hablados_raw <- read_tab_raw(\n  path  = path_idiomas2,\n  sheet = \"8\",\n  stem  = \"idiomas_hablados\"\n)\n```\n\n```{r disaggregate-idiomas-hablados}\nidiomas_hablados <- idiomas_hablados_raw |>\n  disaggregate_tab(last_var = \"area\") |>\n  attach_ine_codes()\n```\n\n### Quick checks\n\n```{r checks-idiomas-hablados}\n#| echo: false\n#| asis: true\ncat(\"Raw rows:               \", nrow(idiomas_hablados$raw),            \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Country rows:           \", nrow(idiomas_hablados$country),        \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept totals:            \", nrow(idiomas_hablados$dept),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept urb+rural:         \", nrow(idiomas_hablados$dept_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province totals:        \", nrow(idiomas_hablados$prov),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province urb+rural:     \", nrow(idiomas_hablados$prov_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality totals:    \", nrow(idiomas_hablados$muni),           \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Municipality urb+rural: \", nrow(idiomas_hablados$muni_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\n```\n\n```{r preview-col-names-idiomas}\n# Verify naming convention: first 12 data column names\nnames(idiomas_hablados$raw)[6:17]\nnames(idiomas_hablados$raw)[80:92]\n```\n\n```{r preview-idiomas-dept}\nidiomas_hablados$dept |>\n  select(departamento, castellano_2024, quechua_2024, aymara_2024, guarani_2024, kabinena_2024) |>\n  arrange(desc(castellano_2024)) |>\n  kbl() |> kable_classic()\n```\n\n### Save language outputs\n\n```{r save-idiomas-hablados}\n#| eval: false\nsave_tab(idiomas_hablados, \"idiomas_hablados\")\n```\n\n------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n## Part 4: Languages Spoken by Sex (`IDIOMAS_2.xlsx`, sheet \"7\")\n\nSource: sheet \"7\" of `IDIOMAS_2.xlsx`. Same language counts as Part 3, but the within-entity breakdown is by sex (Hombres / Mujeres) rather than area. The 5th geo column is `SEXO`; total rows carry the geographic name in that column.\n\n```{r load-idiomas-hablados-s}\n#| message: false\nidiomas_hablados_s_raw <- read_tab_raw(\n  path     = path_idiomas2,\n  sheet    = \"7\",\n  stem     = \"idiomas_hablados_s\",\n  last_var = \"sexo\"\n)\n```\n\n```{r disaggregate-idiomas-hablados-s}\nidiomas_hablados_s <- idiomas_hablados_s_raw |>\n  disaggregate_tab(last_var = \"sexo\") |>\n  attach_ine_codes(last_var = \"sexo\")\n```\n\n### Quick checks\n\n```{r checks-idiomas-hablados-s}\n#| echo: false\n#| asis: true\ncat(\"Raw rows:          \", nrow(idiomas_hablados_s$raw),        \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Country rows:      \", nrow(idiomas_hablados_s$country),    \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept totals:       \", nrow(idiomas_hablados_s$dept),       \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Dept by sex:       \", nrow(idiomas_hablados_s$dept_sexo),  \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province totals:   \", nrow(idiomas_hablados_s$prov),       \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Province by sex:   \", nrow(idiomas_hablados_s$prov_sexo),  \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Muni totals:       \", nrow(idiomas_hablados_s$muni),       \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Muni by sex:       \", nrow(idiomas_hablados_s$muni_sexo),  \"\\n\")\n```\n\n```{r preview-idiomas-s-dept}\nidiomas_hablados_s$dept_sexo |>\n  select(departamento, sexo, castellano_2024, quechua_2024, aymara_2024) |>\n  arrange(departamento, sexo) |>\n  head(18) |>\n  kbl() |> kable_classic()\n```\n\n### Save\n\n```{r save-idiomas-hablados-s}\n#| eval: false\n# Use the shared stem; save_last_var_only keeps only the *_sexo slots,\n# leaving the area-breakdown files from Part 3 untouched.\nsave_tab(idiomas_hablados_s, \"idiomas_hablados\",\n         last_var = \"sexo\", save_last_var_only = TRUE)\n```\n"},"elapsedMs":95}</script></tool-result></div>

Now I see the structure. Sheet 3 has 15 columns:
- Col 1: row_num
- Col 2: nivel
- Col 3: departamento
- Col 4: provincia
- Col 5: municipio
- Col 6: age_area (this is the combined area + age group column)
- Cols 7-15: pop counts (3 years × 3 sex categories = 9 columns)

The age_area column contains:
- Entity name (e.g., "Bolivia") for the total row
- Age groups: "0 a 4", "5 a 9", ..., "95 o más"
- "Urbana" for the urban total followed by the same 20 age groups
- "Rural" for the rural total followed by the same 20 age groups

So each geographic entity has 63 rows total: one total row plus 20 age groups, then the same pattern for urban and rural areas. This is quite different from the other sheets. I need to verify the exact age groups and figure out how to parse this structure correctly.

Within each geographic entity block of 63 rows, the parsing logic would be: when `age_area` matches the entity name, that's the total row; "Urbana" and "Rural" mark area subdivisions with total age; everything else gets assigned as an age group with the area carried forward from the previous marker. This table reports population by quinquennial age group (0–4 through 95+) for each geographic unit, with separate blocks for total, urban, and rural populations. Unlike the other sheets, the 6th column (`age_area`) serves double duty: it carries area markers (\"Urbana\", \"Rural\", or the entity name for totals) **and** age-group labels within each block.\n+\n+Each geographic entity therefore spans 63 rows:\n+\n+- **Total block** (21 rows): entity name, then 20 age groups\n+- **Urbana block** (21 rows): \"Urbana\", then 20 age groups\n+- **Rural block** (21 rows): \"Rural\", then 20 age groups\n+\n+Data columns are simpler than sheet \"1\": population counts only (no percentages), giving 9 data columns (2001 / 2012 / 2024 × total / hombre / mujer).\n+\n+```{r load-population-age}\n+#| message: false\n+age_groups <- c(\n+  \"0 a 4\", \"5 a 9\", \"10 a 14\", \"15 a 19\", \"20 a 24\", \"25 a 29\",\n+  \"30 a 34\", \"35 a 39\", \"40 a 44\", \"45 a 49\", \"50 a 54\", \"55 a 59\",\n+  \"60 a 64\", \"65 a 69\", \"70 a 74\", \"75 a 79\", \"80 a 84\", \"85 a 89\",\n+  \"90 a 94\", \"95 o más\"\n+)\n+\n+raw_age <- read_excel(\n+  \"../bolivia-data/censo_2024/POBLACION.xlsx\",\n+  sheet     = \"3\",\n+  col_names = FALSE,\n+  skip      = 7\n+)\n+\n+age_col_names <- c(\n+  \"row_num\", \"nivel\", \"departamento\", \"provincia\", \"municipio\", \"age_area\",\n+  \"pop_2001_total\", \"pop_2001_hombre\", \"pop_2001_mujer\",\n+  \"pop_2012_total\", \"pop_2012_hombre\", \"pop_2012_mujer\",\n+  \"pop_2024_total\", \"pop_2024_hombre\", \"pop_2024_mujer\"\n+)\n+\n+names(raw_age) <- age_col_names\n+\n+raw_age <- raw_age |>\n+  mutate(\n+    row_num = as.integer(row_num),\n+    across(starts_with(\"pop_\"), as.numeric)\n+  ) |>\n+  filter(!is.na(row_num))\n+```\n+\n+### Parse area and age group\n+\n+The `age_area` column encodes both the area (total / Urbana / Rural) and the age group. Area-marker rows carry the entity name or \"Urbana\" / \"Rural\"; the subsequent 20 rows in each block carry age-group labels. We assign `area` by forward-filling from these markers.\n+\n+```{r parse-area-age}\n+pop_age_raw <- raw_age |>\n+  mutate(\n+    is_age_group = age_area %in% age_groups,\n+    # Area markers: \"Urbana\", \"Rural\", or the entity-name total row\n+    area = case_when(\n+      age_area == \"Urbana\" ~ \"Urbana\",\n+      age_area == \"Rural\"  ~ \"Rural\",\n+      !is_age_group        ~ \"Total\",\n+      TRUE                 ~ NA_character_\n+    )\n+  ) |>\n+  fill(area, .direction = \"down\") |>\n+  mutate(\n+    grupo_edad = if_else(is_age_group, age_area, \"Total\")\n+  ) |>\n+  select(-age_area, -is_age_group) |>\n+  relocate(area, grupo_edad, .after = municipio)\n+```\n+\n+### Disaggregate by geographic level\n+\n+```{r disaggregate-population-age}\n+subdivisions_age <- c(\"Urbana\", \"Rural\")\n+\n+pop_age_country <- pop_age_raw |> filter(nivel == \"País\")\n+\n+pop_age_dept <- pop_age_raw |>\n+  filter(nivel == \"Departamento\", !area %in% subdivisions_age)\n+\n+pop_age_dept_urb_rural <- pop_age_raw |>\n+  filter(nivel == \"Departamento\", area %in% subdivisions_age)\n+\n+pop_age_prov <- pop_age_raw |>\n+  filter(nivel == \"Provincia\", !area %in% subdivisions_age) |>\n+  filter(provincia != \"Territorio Indígena Multiétnico\")\n+\n+pop_age_prov_urb_rural <- pop_age_raw |>\n+  filter(nivel == \"Provincia\", area %in% subdivisions_age) |>\n+  filter(provincia != \"Territorio Indígena Multiétnico\")\n+\n+pop_age_muni <- pop_age_raw |>\n+  filter(nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\", !area %in% subdivisions_age)\n+\n+pop_age_muni_urb_rural <- pop_age_raw |>\n+  filter(nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\", area %in% subdivisions_age)\n+```\n+\n+```{r add-ine-code-population-age}\n+# The add_ine_code helpers expect an \"area\" column; here we already have one\n+# but it holds \"Total\" for the total rows. Temporarily swap it for the helpers.\n+add_ine_code_muni_age <- function(df) {\n+  df |>\n+    left_join(crosswalk_muni, by = c(\"municipio\" = \"muni_cpv2024\",\n+                                     \"departamento\" = \"department\")) |>\n+    relocate(ine_code, municipio, provincia, departamento, area, grupo_edad, nivel)\n+}\n+\n+add_ine_code_prov_age <- function(df) {\n+  df |>\n+    left_join(crosswalk_prov, by = c(\"provincia\" = \"province_cpv2024\",\n+                                     \"departamento\" = \"department\")) |>\n+    relocate(ine_code, provincia, departamento, area, grupo_edad, nivel) |>\n+    select(-municipio)\n+}\n+\n+add_ine_code_dept_age <- function(df) {\n+  df |>\n+    left_join(dep_crosswalk, by = c(\"departamento\" = \"department\")) |>\n+    relocate(id_dep, departamento, area, grupo_edad, nivel) |>\n+    select(-provincia, -municipio)\n+}\n+\n+pop_age_muni           <- add_ine_code_muni_age(pop_age_muni)\n+pop_age_muni_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_muni_age(pop_age_muni_urb_rural)\n+pop_age_prov           <- add_ine_code_prov_age(pop_age_prov)\n+pop_age_prov_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_prov_age(pop_age_prov_urb_rural)\n+pop_age_dept           <- add_ine_code_dept_age(pop_age_dept)\n+pop_age_dept_urb_rural <- add_ine_code_dept_age(pop_age_dept_urb_rural)\n+```\n+\n+### Quick checks\n+\n+```{r checks-population-age}\n+#| echo: false\n+#| asis: true\n+cat(\"Raw rows:               \", nrow(pop_age_raw),            \"\\n\")\n+cat(\"Country rows:           \", nrow(pop_age_country),        \"\\n\")\n+cat(\"Dept totals:            \", nrow(pop_age_dept),           \"\\n\")\n+cat(\"Dept urban+rural rows:  \", nrow(pop_age_dept_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\n+cat(\"Province totals:        \", nrow(pop_age_prov),           \"\\n\")\n+cat(\"Province urban+rural:   \", nrow(pop_age_prov_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\n+cat(\"Municipality totals:    \", nrow(pop_age_muni),           \"\\n\")\n+cat(\"Municipality urb+rural: \", nrow(pop_age_muni_urb_rural), \"\\n\")\n+```\n+\n+```{r preview-population-age-dept}\n+pop_age_dept |>\n+  filter(grupo_edad != \"Total\") |>\n+  select(departamento, grupo_edad, pop_2024_total) |>\n+  head(21) |>\n+  kbl() |> kable_classic()\n+```\n+\n+### Save population by age outputs\n+\n+```{r save-population-age}\n+#| eval: false\n+#| echo: true\n+\n+# ── Spanish ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n+pop_age_es_fix <- function(df) {\n+  df |>\n+    rename_with(~ stringr::str_replace(., \"^pop\", \"pob\"), starts_with(\"pop\"))\n+}\n+\n+saveRDS(pop_age_es_fix(pop_age_raw),            \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_edad_raw.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_es_fix(pop_age_country),        \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_edad_pais.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_es_fix(pop_age_dept),           \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_edad_departamento.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_es_fix(pop_age_dept_urb_rural), \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_edad_departamento_urb_rural.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_es_fix(pop_age_prov),           \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_edad_provincia.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_es_fix(pop_age_prov_urb_rural), \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_edad_provincia_urb_rural.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_es_fix(pop_age_muni),           \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_edad_municipio.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_es_fix(pop_age_muni_urb_rural), \"data/cpv2024/poblacion_edad_municipio_urb_rural.rds\")\n+\n+# ── English ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n+pop_age_en_fix <- function(df) {\n+  df |>\n+    rename(\n+      department  = any_of(\"departamento\"),\n+      province    = any_of(\"provincia\"),\n+      municipality = any_of(\"municipio\"),\n+      level       = any_of(\"nivel\"),\n+      age_group   = grupo_edad\n+    ) |>\n+    rename_with(~ stringr::str_replace(., \"_mujer\", \"_female\"), ends_with(\"mujer\")) |>\n+    rename_with(~ stringr::str_replace(., \"_hombre\", \"_male\"), ends_with(\"hombre\"))\n+}\n+\n+saveRDS(pop_age_en_fix(pop_age_raw),            \"data/cpv2024/population_age_raw.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_en_fix(pop_age_country),        \"data/cpv2024/population_age_country.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_en_fix(pop_age_dept),           \"data/cpv2024/population_age_department.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_en_fix(pop_age_dept_urb_rural), \"data/cpv2024/population_age_department_urb_rural.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_en_fix(pop_age_prov),           \"data/cpv2024/population_age_province.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_en_fix(pop_age_prov_urb_rural), \"data/cpv2024/population_age_province_urb_rural.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_en_fix(pop_age_muni),           \"data/cpv2024/population_age_municipality.rds\")\n+saveRDS(pop_age_en_fix(pop_age_muni_urb_rural), \"data/cpv2024/population_age_municipality_urb_rural.rds\")\n+```\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Everything parses cleanly. The row counts are consistent: 9 departments × 21 = 189, 339 municipalities × ~21 = 7,119 (plus a few TIOCs bringing it to 7,203), and so on.

The new **Part 5** section is now appended to [censo-2024-population.qmd](censo-2024-population.qmd). It:

- Reads sheet "3" of `POBLACION.xlsx` (15 columns: row_num + 5 geo + 9 data)
- Parses the dual-purpose `age_area` column into separate `area` (Total/Urbana/Rural) and `grupo_edad` columns using forward-fill from area markers
- Disaggregates by geographic level, same pattern as Parts 1–2
- Adds INE codes via the existing crosswalks
- Saves 16 RDS files: 8 Spanish (`poblacion_edad_*.rds`) and 8 English (`population_age_*.rds`), with `grupo_edad` → `age_group` and the usual variable translations

The save chunk is `eval: false` like the others. Note the age-specific INE code helpers are defined inline since the existing `add_ine_code_*` functions expect an `area` column used for `relocate` ordering that doesn't quite fit the two-column (`area` + `grupo_edad`) layout here.

<span class="thinking-topic">Understanding the current save pattern</span>



Currently `save_tab(idiomas_hablados, "idiomas_hablados")` saves everything with the Spanish stem. I need to also save English versions with translated variable names and an English stem like `languages_spoken`. Let me check what `save_tab` does and the naming patterns used elsewhere.

The `english_var_name_fix` function renames departamento→department, provincia→province, municipio→municipality, nivel→level, mujer→female, hombre→male.

For the idiomas data, the column names are language names (castellano_2024, quechua_2024, etc.) which are language-neutral enough. 

The geo columns need translation. Looking at how autoidentificacion handles this, they save Spanish files with a Spanish stem and English files with an English stem. I should follow the same pattern for idiomas—keep the Spanish version as `idiomas_hablados_*` and create an English version as `languages_spoken_*` with the geo column names translated.
</thinking><span data-thinking-duration-ms="7144"></span></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_016Nhetd4bmYxYMoHrZUXiof"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 39.9KB, 982 lines, showing lines 636-670 (truncated)]\n   636→# ── attach_ine_codes ...[truncated, total 2009 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 39.9KB, 982 lines, showing lines 636-670 (truncated)]\n# ── attach_ine_codes ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Applies add_ine_code_dept/prov/muni() to matching list slots.\n# last_var: if not \"area\", temporarily renames the column to \"area\" before\n# calling the helpers (which hardcode that name) and renames it back after.\nattach_ine_codes <- function(lst, last_var = \"area\") {\n  needs_rename <- last_var != \"area\"\n  prep   <- function(df) if (needs_rename) rename(df, area = all_of(last_var)) else df\n  unprep <- function(df) if (needs_rename) rename(df, !!last_var := area) else df\n\n  for (key in names(lst)) {\n    if (!is.data.frame(lst[[key]])) next\n    if (startsWith(key, \"dept\")) lst[[key]] <- unprep(add_ine_code_dept(prep(lst[[key]])))\n    if (startsWith(key, \"prov\")) lst[[key]] <- unprep(add_ine_code_prov(prep(lst[[key]])))\n    if (startsWith(key, \"muni\")) lst[[key]] <- unprep(add_ine_code_muni(prep(lst[[key]])))\n  }\n  lst\n}\n\n# ── save_tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Saves each data-frame slot as {dir}/{stem}_{key}.rds.\n#\n# save_last_var_only: if TRUE, only save slots whose key contains last_var\n# (e.g. \"dept_sexo\", \"prov_sexo\", \"muni_sexo\"). Use this when a second\n# breakdown (sex) shares a stem with an existing breakdown (area) to avoid\n# overwriting the totals and the other subdivision's files.\nsave_tab <- function(lst, stem, dir = \"data/cpv2024/\",\n                     last_var = NULL, save_last_var_only = FALSE) {\n  for (key in names(lst)) {\n    if (!is.data.frame(lst[[key]])) next\n    if (save_last_var_only && !is.null(last_var) && !grepl(last_var, key)) next\n    saveRDS(lst[[key]], file.path(dir, paste0(stem, \"_\", key, \".rds\")))\n  }\n  invisible(lst)\n}\n```"},"elapsedMs":77}</script></tool-result></div>

## User

Add a {{population pyramid 2}} template to each municipality in bolivia-muni-generator.qmd. This will involve caculating population percentages for each five-year group out of the total population, with the bins specific to one gender (100% = total population, all male and female percents together add to 100%, values give in % not decimal form). Use data in population_age_municipality.rds

Read the format as follows:
{{Population pyramid 2
|year=2010
|m0=0.7 |m5=1.0 |m10=1.0 |m15=1.0 |m20=1.0 |m25=1.9 |m30=2.3 |m35=3.5 |m40=5.1 |m45=7.8 |m50=7.7 |m55=6.2 |m60=5.7 |m65=3.7 |m70=1.9 |m75=1.7 |m80=0.8 |m85=1.4
|f0=1.1 |f5=0.8 |f10=0.8 |f15=1.1 |f20=1.3 |f25=1.8 |f30=2.0 |f35=2.0 |f40=2.8 |f45=4.2 |f50=5.7 |f55=5.2 |f60=5.8 |f65=3.0 |f70=2.3 |f75=2.1 |f80=1.6 |f85=1.8
|mmax=8.5
|fmax=7.5
|source=Census of X
|sourceref=Census Organization, Title, Date.
}}

Required parameters
From m0 to m85: percentage of those five-year groups of males with respect to the total population.
From f0 to f85: percentage of those five-year groups of females with respect to the total population.

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01PnsWgRjBZfDP1m3waZERCQ"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 3.0KB, 119 lines, showing lines 1-80 (truncated)]\n     1→---\n     2→title: \"Bolivia...[truncated, total 2879 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 3.0KB, 119 lines, showing lines 1-80 (truncated)]\n---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tablas del Concejo Municipal\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext listo para copiar a Wikipedia\"\nformat:\n  html:\n    code-fold: true\n    toc: false\n    self-contained: true\n---\n\n```{r}\n#| label: setup\n#| include: false\n\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(purrr)\nlibrary(htmltools)\n\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\nsource(\"src/gh-copy-assets.R\")\n\nmuni_lookup  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nconcejo_data <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_concejo_comb.rds\"))\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: helpers\n#| include: false\n\n# Renders a wikitext copy block (button + visible pre + hidden textarea)\nwikitext_block <- function(id, label, content) {\n  tagList(\n    tags$button(\n      class = \"copy-btn\",\n      style = \"margin-bottom: 6px;\",\n      onclick = sprintf(\"copyTableToClipboard('%s', this)\", id),\n      label\n    ),\n    tags$pre(\n      tags$code(content),\n      style = paste(\n        \"background-color: #f6f8fa; padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px;\",\n        \"overflow-x: auto; font-size: 11px; white-space: pre; margin: 0;\"\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$textarea(\n      id = id,\n      style = \"position: absolute; left: -9999px; height: 0; width: 0; overflow: hidden;\",\n      readonly = TRUE,\n      content\n    )\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders a collapsible <details> block for one municipality.\n# Left panel: English kable preview. Right panel: Spanish wikitext + copy button.\nmuni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, council_wikitext, concejo) {\n  kbl <- build_council_kable_en(id_muni, data = concejo)\n\n  tagList(\n    tags$details(\n      tags$summary(\n        tags$strong(muni_name),\n        tags$span(\n          paste0(\" \\u2014 \", department),\n          style = \"color: #586069; font-weight: normal;\"\n        ),\n        tags$code(\n          paste0(\" [\", id_muni, \"]\"),\n          style = \"font-size: 11px; color: #999; margin-left: 6px;\"\n        )\n      ),\n      tags$div(\n        style = \"display:flex; gap:14px; align-items:flex-start; margin-top:8px; font-size:12px;\",\n        tags$div(style = \"flex:1 1 0; min-width:0; overflow-x:auto;\", kbl),\n        tags$div(\n          style = \"flex:1 1 0; min-width:0;\",\n          wikitext_block(\n            id      = paste0(\"concejo_\", id_muni),\n            label   = \"Copiar tabla del concejo\","},"elapsedMs":2001}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01YH1k23oiZDdVso5TSWFYxN"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 3.0KB, 119 lines, showing lines 80-119]\n    80→            label   = \"Copiar tabla ...[truncated, total 1200 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 3.0KB, 119 lines, showing lines 80-119]\n            label   = \"Copiar tabla del concejo\",\n            content = council_wikitext\n          )\n        )\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$hr(style = \"border:none; border-top:1px solid #e1e4e8; margin:4px 0;\")\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders all municipality blocks sorted by id_muni\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup, concejo) {\n  ids <- lookup |>\n    filter(id_muni %in% unique(concejo$id_muni)) |>\n    arrange(id_muni) |>\n    select(id_muni, muni_anexo, department)\n\n  blocks <- pmap(ids, function(id_muni, muni_anexo, department) {\n    wt <- build_council_wikitable_es(id_muni, data = concejo)\n    muni_block(id_muni, muni_anexo, department, wt, concejo)\n  })\n\n  tagList(blocks)\n}\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: council-tables\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_muni_blocks(muni_lookup, concejo_data)\n```\n"},"elapsedMs":1837}</script></tool-result></div>

<span class="thinking-topic">Mapping age groups to template parameters</span>



The Wikipedia template uses m0-m85 and f0-f85 (18 bins each). The data has 20 age groups (0-4 through 95+). The template stops at 85, so I need to combine 85-89, 90-94, and 95+ into m85/f85.

Template bins: 0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35, 40, 45, 50, 55, 60, 65, 70, 75, 80, 85

For the final bin, m85 and f85 will represent the combined percentage of males and females aged 85 and older. Now I'm building a function that loads the data filtered to the total area, calculates the total population, then computes male and female percentages for each 5-year age group, combining the three oldest groups into the final template bin.

I also need to check the template format for mmax and fmax—those scale the chart—plus the source and sourceref fields for citations.

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01X922sDHaXPh3obhBw5YZ7H"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","old_string":"muni_lookup  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nconcejo_data <- re...[truncated, total 151 chars]","new_string":"muni_lookup  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nconcejo_data <- re...[truncated, total 241 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -21,6 +21,7 @@\n \n muni_lookup  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\n concejo_data <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_concejo_comb.rds\"))\n+pop_age_muni <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"population_age_municipality.rds\"))\n ```\n \n ```{r}\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01LiveuChUVpvkWGiEqoa7HS"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","old_string":"# Renders all municipality blocks sorted by id_muni\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup, concejo) {","new_string":"# Build a {{Population pyramid 2}} wikitext template for one municipality.\nbuild_pop_pyram...[truncated, total 1707 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -88,6 +88,56 @@\n   )\n }\n \n+# Build a {{Population pyramid 2}} wikitext template for one municipality.\n+build_pop_pyramid <- function(id, pop_data) {\n+  d <- pop_data |>\n+    filter(ine_code == id, area == \"Total\", age_group != \"Total\")\n+  if (nrow(d) == 0) return(NULL)\n+\n+  total_pop <- pop_data |>\n+    filter(ine_code == id, area == \"Total\", age_group == \"Total\") |>\n+    pull(pop_2024_total)\n+\n+  # Map age groups to template bin numbers; 85+ groups all map to bin 85\n+  bin_map <- c(\n+    \"0 a 4\" = 0, \"5 a 9\" = 5, \"10 a 14\" = 10, \"15 a 19\" = 15,\n+    \"20 a 24\" = 20, \"25 a 29\" = 25, \"30 a 34\" = 30, \"35 a 39\" = 35,\n+    \"40 a 44\" = 40, \"45 a 49\" = 45, \"50 a 54\" = 50, \"55 a 59\" = 55,\n+    \"60 a 64\" = 60, \"65 a 69\" = 65, \"70 a 74\" = 70, \"75 a 79\" = 75,\n+    \"80 a 84\" = 80, \"85 a 89\" = 85, \"90 a 94\" = 85, \"95 o m\\u00e1s\" = 85\n+  )\n+\n+  d <- d |>\n+    mutate(bin = bin_map[age_group]) |>\n+    summarise(\n+      male   = sum(pop_2024_male,   na.rm = TRUE),\n+      female = sum(pop_2024_female, na.rm = TRUE),\n+      .by = bin\n+    ) |>\n+    mutate(\n+      m_pct = round(male   / total_pop * 100, 1),\n+      f_pct = round(female / total_pop * 100, 1)\n+    ) |>\n+    arrange(bin)\n+\n+  m_parts <- paste0(\"|m\", d$bin, \"=\", d$m_pct, collapse = \" \")\n+  f_parts <- paste0(\"|f\", d$bin, \"=\", d$f_pct, collapse = \" \")\n+  mmax <- ceiling(max(d$m_pct) * 2) / 2\n+  fmax <- ceiling(max(d$f_pct) * 2) / 2\n+\n+  paste0(\n+    \"{{Population pyramid 2\\n\",\n+    \"|year=2024\\n\",\n+    m_parts, \"\\n\",\n+    f_parts, \"\\n\",\n+    \"|mmax=\", mmax, \"\\n\",\n+    \"|fmax=\", fmax, \"\\n\",\n+    \"|source=2024 Bolivian census\\n\",\n+    \"|sourceref=INE, Censo de Poblaci\\u00f3n y Vivienda 2024.\\n\",\n+    \"}}\"\n+  )\n+}\n+\n # Renders all municipality blocks sorted by id_muni\n render_muni_blocks <- function(lookup, concejo) {\n   ids <- lookup |>\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

The changes to [bolivia-muni-generator.qmd](bolivia-muni-generator.qmd):

1. **Setup**: loads `population_age_municipality.rds`
2. **`build_pop_pyramid()`**: computes per-bin percentages (% of total pop), collapses 85–89 / 90–94 / 95+ into the `m85`/`f85` bin, rounds `mmax`/`fmax` up to nearest 0.5
3. **`muni_block()`**: gains an optional third panel for the pyramid wikitext with its own copy button
4. **`render_muni_blocks()`**: passes `pop_data` through and calls `build_pop_pyramid()` per municipality

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01Qd8pa9n6kiEy2SB2uBGoaJ"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 33.5KB, 979 lines, showing lines 416-545 (truncated)]\n   416→build_language_wikitab...[truncated, total 6255 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 33.5KB, 979 lines, showing lines 416-545 (truncated)]\nbuild_language_wikitable_en <- function(id_muni, lang_data, pop_data,\n                                         caption = \"Languages spoken\",\n                                         source = NULL, source_refs = NULL) {\n  row <- lang_data |> dplyr::filter(ine_code == id_muni,\n                                     nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\") |>\n         dplyr::slice(1)\n  if (nrow(row) == 0) return(\"\")\n  pop_row  <- pop_data |> dplyr::filter(id_muni == .env$id_muni) |> dplyr::slice(1)\n  if (nrow(pop_row) == 0) return(\"\")\n  pop_2024 <- pop_row$pop_2024\n  pop_2012 <- pop_row$pop_2012\n\n  # Native + national language columns only (exclude _ext_ foreign languages)\n  cols_2024 <- names(lang_data) |>\n    stringr::str_subset(\"_2024$\") |>\n    stringr::str_subset(\"_ext_\", negate = TRUE)\n  cols_2012 <- stringr::str_replace(cols_2024, \"_2024$\", \"_2012\")\n  # Keep only pairs that exist in 2012 as well\n  valid     <- cols_2012 %in% names(lang_data)\n  cols_2024 <- cols_2024[valid]; The `pop_muni` data frame correctly has both `pop_2024` and `pop_2012` columns (line 98), sourced from `population_municipality.rds`. And in the language functions, `pct_2012 = n_2012 / pop_2012` (line 442) uses the 2012 denominator.

The denominators are correct — 2012 speakers are divided by `pop_2012` and 2024 speakers by `pop_2024`. Both come from the same population table with year-specific totals.

margin:4px 0 4px; color:#333;\"),\n        tags$p(HTML(council_disp$html),  style = \"font-size:12px; margin:0 0 0;    color:#333;\"),\n        refs_footnotes(all_intro_refs),\n        tags$textarea(\n          id       = paste0(\"intro_en_\", id_muni),\n          style    = \"position:absolute; min-width:0;\",\n          wikitext_block(\n            id      = paste0(\"autoident_en_\", id_muni),\n            label   = \"Copy identity table\",\n            content = build_autoident_wikitable_en(id_muni, auto_data, pop_muni)\n          )\n        )\n      ),\n      # Side-by-side: language kable | wikitable\n      tags$div(\n        style = \"display:flex; gap:14px; align-items:flex-start; font-size:12px; margin-bottom:8px;\",\n        tags$div(\n          style = \"flex:1 1 0; min-width:0; overflow-x:auto;\",\n          build_language_kable_en(id_muni, lang_data, pop_muni)\n        ),\n        tags$div(\n          style = \"flex:1 1 0; min-width:0;\",\n          wikitext_block(\n            id      = paste0(\"lang_en_\", id_muni),\n            label   = \"Copy language table\",\n            content = build_language_wikitable_en(id_muni, lang_data, pop_muni)\n          )\n        )\n      ),\n      # Side-by-side: council kable | wikitable\n      tags$div(\n        style = \"display:flex; border-top:1px solid #e1e4e8; margin:4px 0;\")\n  )\n}\n\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup, concejo) {\n  ids <- lookup |>\n    filter(id_muni %in% unique(concejo$id_muni)) |>\n    arrange(id_muni) |>\n    select(id_muni, muni_anexo, department)\n\n  tagList(pmap(ids, function(id_muni, muni_anexo, department) {\n    muni_block(id_muni, muni_anexo, department, concejo)\n  }))\n}\n```\n\n```{r source-data}\nsource_wikitext_en = \"Supreme Electoral Tribunal.\"\nsource_wikitext_es = \"Tribunal Supremo Electoral.\"\n\nsource_ref_en  <- \"{{Cite report| publisher = Órgano Electoral Plurinacional| last1 = Órgano Electoral Plurinacional| last2 = Tribunal Supremo Electoral| title = Publicación de resultados Elecciones Subnacionales 2026: Elección de Autoridades Políticas Departamentales Regionales y Municipales 2026 (Primera y Segunda Vuelta Electoral)| series = Separata de información pública| date = 2026-04-23}}\"\nsource_ref_es <- source_ref_en\n\nsource_ref_with_page_number <- function(page_number, source_ref) {\n  source_ref_minus_end <- sub(\"\\\\}\\\\}$\", \"\", source_ref)\n  paste0(source_ref_minus_end, \" | page = \", page_number, \" }}\")\n}\n\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: council-tables\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_muni_blocks(muni_lookup, concejo_data)\n```\n"},"elapsedMs":80}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01GGBBxa6eTDRFbEXjnfsxqW"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"write","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","content":"---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tablas del Concejo Municipal\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext listo para co...[truncated, total 3086 chars]","overwrite":true},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tablas del Concejo Municipal\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext listo para copiar a Wikipedia\"\nformat:\n  html:\n    code-fold: true\n    toc: false\n    self-contained: true\n---\n\n```{r}\n#| label: setup\n#| include: false\n\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(purrr)\nlibrary(htmltools)\n\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\nsource(\"src/gh-copy-assets.R\")\n\nmuni_lookup  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nconcejo_data <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_concejo_comb.rds\"))\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: helpers\n#| include: false\n\n# Renders a wikitext copy block (button + visible pre + hidden textarea)\nwikitext_block <- function(id, label, content) {\n  tagList(\n    tags$button(\n      class = \"copy-btn\",\n      style = \"margin-bottom: 6px;\",\n      onclick = sprintf(\"copyTableToClipboard('%s', this)\", id),\n      label\n    ),\n    tags$pre(\n      tags$code(content),\n      style = paste(\n        \"background-color: #f6f8fa; padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px;\",\n        \"overflow-x: auto; font-size: 11px; white-space: pre; margin: 0;\"\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$textarea(\n      id = id,\n      style = \"position: absolute; left: -9999px; height: 0; width: 0; overflow: hidden;\",\n      readonly = TRUE,\n      content\n    )\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders a collapsible <details> block for one municipality.\n# Left panel: English kable preview. Right panel: Spanish wikitext + copy button.\nmuni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, council_wikitext, concejo) {\n  kbl <- build_council_kable_en(id_muni, data = concejo)\n\n  tagList(\n    tags$details(\n      tags$summary(\n        tags$strong(muni_name),\n        tags$span(\n          paste0(\" \\u2014 \", department),\n          style = \"color: #586069; font-weight: normal;\"\n        ),\n        tags$code(\n          paste0(\" [\", id_muni, \"]\"),\n          style = \"font-size: 11px; color: #999; margin-left: 6px;\"\n        )\n      ),\n      tags$div(\n        style = \"display:flex; gap:14px; align-items:flex-start; margin-top:8px; font-size:12px;\",\n        tags$div(style = \"flex:1 1 0; min-width:0; overflow-x:auto;\", kbl),\n        tags$div(\n          style = \"flex:1 1 0; min-width:0;\",\n          wikitext_block(\n            id      = paste0(\"concejo_\", id_muni),\n            label   = \"Copiar tabla del concejo\",\n            content = council_wikitext\n          )\n        )\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$hr(style = \"border:none; border-top:1px solid #e1e4e8; margin:4px 0;\")\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders all municipality blocks sorted by id_muni\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup, concejo) {\n  ids <- lookup |>\n    filter(id_muni %in% unique(concejo$id_muni)) |>\n    arrange(id_muni) |>\n    select(id_muni, muni_anexo, department)\n\n  blocks <- pmap(ids, function(id_muni, muni_anexo, department) {\n    wt <- build_council_wikitable_es(id_muni, data = concejo)\n    muni_block(id_muni, muni_anexo, department, wt, concejo)\n  })\n\n  tagList(blocks)\n}\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: council-tables\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_muni_blocks(muni_lookup, concejo_data)\n```\n","language":"markdown"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_019Y8pPLt4uQFLy7oWB3dU1p"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 20.8KB, 555 lines, showing lines 1-110 (truncated)]\n     1→---\n     2→title: \"Boliv...[truncated, total 5276 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 20.8KB, 555 lines, showing lines 1-110 (truncated)]\n---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Municipal Council Tables\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext ready to copy to Wikipedia\"\nformat:\n  html:\n    code-fold: true\n    toc: false\n    self-contained: true\n---\n\n::: {style=\"font-size: 11px;\"}\n\n## Template\n\nEach municipality section contains three copyable blocks.\n\n---\n\n**Copy lead wikitext** — one to three sentences:\n\n> `'''[Municipality] Municipality'''` is the `[ordinal]` [[Municipalities of Bolivia\\|municipal section]] of the `[[Province Province]]` in the `[[Department Department]]` in `[[Bolivia]]`.\n\n*[If the municipality is the 1st or 2nd most populous in Bolivia:]*\n> It is the `[most / second most]` populous municipality in `[[Bolivia]]`.\n\n*[If not nationally top-2, but 1st or 2nd most populous in its department:]*\n> It is the `[most / second most]` populous municipality in `[[Department Department]]`.\n\n*[If not department top-2, and most populous in a province with 3 or more municipalities:]*\n> It is the most populous municipality in `[[Province Province]]`.\n\n*[If the only municipality in its province:]*\n> `[Municipality]` Municipality is the only municipality in `[[Province Province]]` and its boundaries are identical.\n\n*[If one of two municipalities in its province — four variants based on relative area and population:]*\n\n| Larger area? | More populous? | Sentence |\n|---|---|---|\n| Yes | Yes | `[Municipality]` Municipality is the larger and more populous of the two municipalities in `[[Province Province]]`. |\n| Yes | No | `[Municipality]` Municipality covers the larger area of the two municipalities in `[[Province Province]]`, but it is less populous. |\n| No | Yes | `[Municipality]` Municipality is the more populous of the two municipalities in `[[Province Province]]`, but it covers a smaller area. |\n| No | No | `[Municipality]` Municipality is the smaller and less populous of the two municipalities in `[[Province Province]]`. |\n\n*[Omitted for provinces with 3 or more municipalities.]*\n\n---\n\n**Copy government wikitext** — two paragraphs:\n\n> The Mayor of `[Municipality]` is `[Name]`, who was elected in the [March 2026 elections](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Bolivian_regional_elections).\n\n> `[Municipality]` Municipality has `[5 / 7 / 9 / 11]` council seats, as do all `[municipalities with populations up to 15,000 / between 15,001 and 50,000 / between 50,001 and 75,000 / over 75,000 / department capitals]`. *[IOC municipalities only:]* One of those seats is an Indigenous seat assigned by the `[pueblo]` according to its own norms and procedures.\n\n---\n\n**Copy council table** — a wikitable of council members, one row per seat/type:\n\n```\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n! Seat !! Type !! Name !! Party / Political Organization\n|-\n| rowspan=\"2\" | [N] || Member || [Name] || rowspan=\"2\" | [Party]\n|-\n| Alternate || ''[Alternate name]''\n...\n|}\n```\n\n---\n\n:::\n\n\n```{r}\n#| label: setup\n#| include: false\n\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(purrr)\nlibrary(htmltools)\nlibrary(english)\n\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\nsource(\"src/gh-copy-assets.R\")\n\nmuni_lookup       <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nprov_lookup       <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"prov_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nmuni_roster       <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_roster.rds\"))\nconcejo_data      <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_concejo_comb.rds\"))\nconcejal_summary  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"concejal_summary.rds\"))\nmuni_concejal_ioc <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_concejal_ioc.rds\"))\nalcaldes          <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"alcaldes.rds\"))\n\nroster_pages <- muni_roster |> select(id_muni, page)\n\n# Expected seat counts by census population — mirrors cleaning script section 3b\npop_muni         <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"population_municipality.rds\")) |>\n  select(id_muni = ine_code, pop_2024 = pop_2024_total, pop_2012 = pop_2012_total)\n\nlang_data  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"idiomas_hablados_muni.rds\"))\nauto_data  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"autoidentificacion_municipio.rds\"))\ndept_capital_ids <- paste0(sprintf(\"%02d\", 1:9), \"0101\")\n\n# Municipality areas from admin3 boundary file\narea_muni <- readxl::read_excel(here::here(\"maps\", \"bol_admin_boundaries.xlsx\"),\n                                  sheet = \"bol_admin3\") |>\n  mutate(id_muni = stringr::str_sub(adm3_pcode, 3, 8)) |>\n  select(id_muni, area_sqkm)\n\n# Add id_prov if not already present, then build province-context lookup"},"elapsedMs":81}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01XTwi8LQy69Cc1G1DXP7AKf"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 20.9KB, 556 lines, showing lines 145-204 (truncated)]\n   145→  )\n   146→```\n   147→...[truncated, total 2478 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 20.9KB, 556 lines, showing lines 145-204 (truncated)]\n  )\n```\n\n\n```{r}\n#| label: helpers\n#| include: false\n\n# Process wikitext for in-page display:\n#   - <ref>...</ref>  →  superscript [refN] (numbered from ref_offset + 1)\n#   - [[Target|Disp]] →  HTML anchor\n#   - everything else HTML-escaped so wikitext syntax shows literally\n# Returns list(html = character, refs = character vector of ref contents).\nprocess_for_display <- function(text, ref_offset = 0L) {\n  refs <- character(0)\n\n  # Step 1: swap <ref>...</ref> for safe placeholder tokens\n  processed <- stringr::str_replace_all(text, \"<ref>(.*?)</ref>\", function(m) {\n    n    <- length(refs) + 1L\n    refs <<- c(refs, stringr::str_match(m, \"<ref>(.*?)</ref>\")[1, 2])\n    sprintf(\"REFSUP_%d_END\", n + ref_offset)\n  })\n\n  # Step 2: HTML-escape (turns < > & into entities, leaves placeholders intact)\n  escaped <- htmltools::htmlEscape(processed)\n\n  # Step 3: convert [[wikilinks]] to anchors\n  escaped <- stringr::str_replace_all(\n    escaped, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^|\\\\]]+)\\\\|([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\",\n    function(m) {\n      p <- stringr::str_match(m, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^|\\\\]]+)\\\\|([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\")\n      paste0('<a href=\"', wikipedia_url_for(p[, 2]), '\" target=\"_blank\">', p[, 3], \"</a>\")\n    }\n  )\n  escaped <- stringr::str_replace_all(\n    escaped, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\",\n    function(m) {\n      tgt <- stringr::str_match(m, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\")[, 2]\n      paste0('<a href=\"', wikipedia_url_for(tgt), '\" target=\"_blank\">', tgt, \"</a>\")\n    }\n  )\n\n  # Step 4: restore placeholders as blue superscripts\n  for (i in seq_along(refs)) {\n    n_abs <- i + ref_offset\n    escaped <- stringr::str_replace_all(\n      escaped,\n      sprintf(\"REFSUP_%d_END\", n_abs),\n      sprintf('<sup style=\"color:#06c;font-size:9px;\">[ref%d]</sup>', n_abs)\n    )\n  }\n\n  list(html = escaped, refs = refs)\n}\n\n# Render a footnotes section below a display block.\nrefs_footnotes <- function(refs, ref_offset = 0L) {\n  if (length(refs) == 0) return(NULL)\n  tags$div(\n    style = \"margin-top:5px; padding-top:4px; border-top:1px solid #e1e4e8;\","},"elapsedMs":66}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01D18BZcpAM9FNZ5ECCjWxpx"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"read","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","offset":204,"limit":60},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd:204-263","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd:204"}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01D18BZcpAM9FNZ5ECCjWxpx"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 20.9KB, 556 lines, showing lines 204-263 (truncated)]\n   204→    style = \"margin-to...[truncated, total 2570 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 20.9KB, 556 lines, showing lines 204-263 (truncated)]\n    style = \"margin-top:5px; padding-top:4px; border-top:1px solid #e1e4e8;\",\n    lapply(seq_along(refs), function(i) {\n      tags$p(\n        tags$b(paste0(\"ref\", i + ref_offset, \":\")), \" \", refs[[i]],\n        style = \"font-size:10px; font-family:monospace; margin:1px 0; color:#555; word-break:break-all;\"\n      )\n    })\n  )\n}\n\n# Wikitext copy block: button + display (with ref superscripts) + hidden textarea.\nwikitext_block <- function(id, label, content) {\n  disp <- process_for_display(content)\n  tagList(\n    tags$button(\n      class = \"copy-btn\",\n      style = \"margin-bottom:6px;\",\n      onclick = sprintf(\"copyTableToClipboard('%s', this)\", id),\n      label\n    ),\n    tags$pre(\n      HTML(disp$html),\n      style = paste(\n        \"background-color:#f6f8fa; padding:10px; border-radius:6px;\",\n        \"overflow-x:auto; font-size:11px; white-space:pre; margin:0;\"\n      )\n    ),\n    refs_footnotes(disp$refs),\n    tags$textarea(\n      id = id,\n      style = \"position:absolute; left:-9999px; height:0; width:0; overflow:hidden;\",\n      readonly = TRUE,\n      content\n    )\n  )\n}\n\n# Citation for INE 2024 census population data, appended to all population sentences\n.ine_pop_ref <- paste0(\n  \"<ref>{{Cite\",\n  \"| last = Instituto Nacional de Estadística\",\n  \"| title = Población censada e indicadores demográficos: Tabulados por Municipio/TIOC\",\n  \"| url = https://nube.ine.gob.bo/index.php/s/0OrfPjzMB16fATQ/download\",\n  \"| date = 2025}}</ref>\"\n)\n\n# Compose a population-rank sentence for municipalities that are among the most\n# populous nationally (top 2), in their department (top 2), or in their province\n# (rank 1, only for provinces with 3+ municipalities). Returns NULL when none apply.\ncompose_pop_rank_sentence_en <- function(id_muni, muni_pc) {\n  row <- muni_pc[muni_pc$id_muni == id_muni, ]\n\n  # National top-2\n  if (!is.na(row$nat_pop_rank) && row$nat_pop_rank <= 2) {\n    ord <- if (row$nat_pop_rank == 1) \"most\" else \"second most\"\n    return(paste0(\"It is the \", ord, \" populous municipality in [[Bolivia]].\",\n                  .ine_pop_ref))\n  }\n\n  # Department top-2"},"elapsedMs":47}</script></tool-result></div>
