<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01PQei9mQB1dXZ6WDcfDkokE"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 20.9KB, 556 lines, showing lines 380-404 (truncated)]\n   380→\n   381→  ioc_rows <- ...[truncated, total 1111 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 20.9KB, 556 lines, showing lines 380-404 (truncated)]\n\n  ioc_rows <- ioc_data[ioc_data$id_muni == id_muni, ]\n  if (nrow(ioc_rows) > 0) {\n    pueblo <- stringr::str_to_title(ioc_rows$pueblo[1])\n    s2 <- paste0(\n      \"One of those seats is an Indigenous seat assigned by the \",\n      pueblo, \" according to its own norms and procedures.\"\n    )\n    paste(s1, s2)\n  } else {\n    s1\n  }\n}\n\nmuni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, concejo) {\n  page <- roster_pages$page[roster_pages$id_muni == id_muni]\n  src  <- source_ref_with_page_number(page, source_ref_en)\n\n  # Lead sentence\n  lead_wt   <- compose_lead_sentence_en(id_muni, muni_lookup, prov_lookup)\n  lead_disp <- process_for_display(lead_wt)\n\n  # Wikitext for both government paragraphs (combined into one copy target)\n  mayor_wt   <- compose_mayor_sentence_en(id_muni, muni_name, alcaldes, page)\n  council_wt <- compose_council_sentence_en(id_muni, muni_name,"},"elapsedMs":34}</script></tool-result></div>

Now I'll add `build_pop_pyramid` right before `muni_block`, and integrate it into the block and render function.

<div><tool-call id="toolu_014spFyQkn1VdRZ26ysqBm3W"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"muni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, concejo) {","new_string":"# Build a {{Population pyramid 2}} wikitext template for one municipality.\nbuild_pop_pyram...[truncated, total 1671 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -391,6 +391,56 @@\n   }\n }\n \n+# Build a {{Population pyramid 2}} wikitext template for one municipality.\n+build_pop_pyramid <- function(id, pop_data) {\n+  d <- pop_data |>\n+    filter(ine_code == id, area == \"Total\", age_group != \"Total\")\n+  if (nrow(d) == 0) return(NULL)\n+\n+  total_pop <- pop_data |>\n+    filter(ine_code == id, area == \"Total\", age_group == \"Total\") |>\n+    pull(pop_2024_total)\n+\n+  # Map age groups to template bin numbers; 85+ groups all map to bin 85\n+  bin_map <- c(\n+    \"0 a 4\" = 0, \"5 a 9\" = 5, \"10 a 14\" = 10, \"15 a 19\" = 15,\n+    \"20 a 24\" = 20, \"25 a 29\" = 25, \"30 a 34\" = 30, \"35 a 39\" = 35,\n+    \"40 a 44\" = 40, \"45 a 49\" = 45, \"50 a 54\" = 50, \"55 a 59\" = 55,\n+    \"60 a 64\" = 60, \"65 a 69\" = 65, \"70 a 74\" = 70, \"75 a 79\" = 75,\n+    \"80 a 84\" = 80, \"85 a 89\" = 85, \"90 a 94\" = 85, \"95 o m\\u00e1s\" = 85\n+  )\n+\n+  d <- d |>\n+    mutate(bin = bin_map[age_group]) |>\n+    summarise(\n+      male   = sum(pop_2024_male,   na.rm = TRUE),\n+      female = sum(pop_2024_female, na.rm = TRUE),\n+      .by = bin\n+    ) |>\n+    mutate(\n+      m_pct = round(male   / total_pop * 100, 1),\n+      f_pct = round(female / total_pop * 100, 1)\n+    ) |>\n+    arrange(bin)\n+\n+  m_parts <- paste0(\"|m\", d$bin, \"=\", d$m_pct, collapse = \" \")\n+  f_parts <- paste0(\"|f\", d$bin, \"=\", d$f_pct, collapse = \" \")\n+  mmax <- ceiling(max(d$m_pct) * 2) / 2\n+  fmax <- ceiling(max(d$f_pct) * 2) / 2\n+\n+  paste0(\n+    \"{{Population pyramid 2\\n\",\n+    \"|year=2024\\n\",\n+    m_parts, \"\\n\",\n+    f_parts, \"\\n\",\n+    \"|mmax=\", mmax, \"\\n\",\n+    \"|fmax=\", fmax, \"\\n\",\n+    \"|source=2024 Bolivian census\\n\",\n+    \"|sourceref=INE, Censo de Poblaci\\u00f3n y Vivienda 2024.\\n\",\n+    \"}}\"\n+  )\n+}\n+\n muni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, concejo) {\n   page <- roster_pages$page[roster_pages$id_muni == id_muni]\n   src  <- source_ref_with_page_number(page, source_ref_en)\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_012es8iTKPXbbpaebgFUtqnQ"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"lang_data    <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"idiomas_hablados_muni.rds\"))\nauto_d...[truncated, total 321 chars]","new_string":"lang_data    <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"idiomas_hablados_muni.rds\"))\nauto_d...[truncated, total 949 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -104,6 +104,24 @@\n pop_age_muni <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"population_age_municipality.rds\"))\n dept_capital_ids <- paste0(sprintf(\"%02d\", 1:9), \"0101\")\n \n+# Wikipedia presence: which municipalities have an English article?\n+wiki_presence <- bo_municipality_wiki_presence$data |>\n+  left_join(\n+    readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"municipalities_wd_ine.rds\")) |>\n+      select(qid, ine_code),\n+    by = \"qid\"\n+  ) |>\n+  filter(!is.na(ine_code))\n+\n+munis_with_enwiki <- wiki_presence |> filter(en > 0) |> pull(ine_code)\n+\n+# Filter muni_lookup based on params$wiki_filter\n+if (params$wiki_filter == \"existing\") {\n+  muni_lookup <- muni_lookup |> filter(id_muni %in% munis_with_enwiki)\n+} else if (params$wiki_filter == \"new\") {\n+  muni_lookup <- muni_lookup |> filter(!id_muni %in% munis_with_enwiki)\n+}\n+\n # Municipality areas from admin3 boundary file\n area_muni <- readxl::read_excel(here::here(\"maps\", \"bol_admin_boundaries.xlsx\"),\n                                   sheet = \"bol_admin3\") |>\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_0175mbpfKvNJsvmFPSfB6tje"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"```{r}\n#| label: council-tables\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_muni_blocks(muni_lookup, concejo_data)\n```","new_string":"```{r}\n#| label: filter-summary\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nn_total <- nrow(muni_looku...[truncated, total 534 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -632,6 +632,19 @@\n ```\n \n ```{r}\n+#| label: filter-summary\n+#| echo: false\n+#| results: asis\n+n_total <- nrow(muni_lookup |> filter(id_muni %in% unique(concejo_data$id_muni)))\n+filter_label <- switch(params$wiki_filter,\n+  existing = \"with an existing English Wikipedia article\",\n+  new      = \"without an English Wikipedia article (new)\",\n+  \"all (no filter applied)\"\n+)\n+cat(sprintf(\"**Rendering %d municipalities %s.**\\n\\n\", n_total, filter_label))\n+```\n+\n+```{r}\n #| label: council-tables\n #| echo: false\n #| results: asis\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_0175mbpfKvNJsvmFPSfB6tje"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"Updated bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd"},"elapsedMs":369}</script></tool-result></div>

The file now accepts a `wiki_filter` parameter with three options:

- `"existing"` — only municipalities that already have an English Wikipedia article (193)
- `"new"` — only those without one (148)
- `"all"` — no filter (default, backward-compatible)

You can render with a specific filter via:

```r
quarto::quarto_render("bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd", 
                      execute_params = list(wiki_filter = "new"))
```

The filter joins `bo_municipality_wiki_presence$data` to `municipalities_wd_ine.rds` on `qid` to get `ine_code`, then subsets `muni_lookup` early so it flows through the rest of the pipeline unchanged. A summary line at the top of the output states which subset and how many municipalities are included.


<span class="suggestion">Render with `wiki_filter = "new"` to verify the 148-municipality subset works correctly.</span>


<span class="suggestion">Add the same `wiki_filter` parameter to the Spanish generator if needed.</span>

Here is my guidance:

1. Basics generated from the data structure itself. Arguably, no sourcing is needed here.
```
|official_name          = Vacas 
...
|settlement_type        = Municipality 
...
|subdivision_type       = Country
|subdivision_name       = [[Image:Flag of Bolivia.svg|25px]] [[Bolivia]]
|subdivision_type1      = [[Departments of Bolivia|Department]]
|subdivision_name1      = [[Cochabamba Department]]
|subdivision_type2      = [[Provinces of Bolivia|Province]]
|subdivision_name2      = [[Arani Province]]
...
|timezone               = BOT
|utc_offset             = -4
|area_code              =  30502
```
2. Cantons: Note that cantons are no longer formally legal, but remain recognized. A nested list of cantons is already on Wikipedia, but also these may need updating/dividing where new municipalities have been created.
```
|subdivision_type4      = [[Cantons of Bolivia|Cantons]]
|subdivision_name4      = 1
```
3. Population: 2024 Census data is available, reformatted as tidy, and deployed in cpv2024-population-quickstatements.qmd. This gives total, urban, rural populations. Populations by ethnicity are also available in processed census data.
```
<!-- Population   ----------------------->

(params$wiki_filter == \"existing\") {\n  muni_lookup <- muni_lookup |> filter(id_muni %in% munis_with_enwiki)\n} else if (params$wiki_filter == \"new\") {\n  muni_lookup <- muni_lookup |> filter(!id_muni %in% munis_with_enwiki)\n}\n\n# Add id_prov to muni_lookup\nmuni_lookup <- muni_lookup |>\n  mutate(id_prov = stringr::str_sub(id_muni, 1, 4))\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: helpers\n#| include: false\n\n# ── Coordinate formatter ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Converts decimal degrees to {{coord|d|m|s|N/S|d|m|s|E/W|...}} format\nfmt_coord <- function(lat, lon) {\n  dec_to_dms <- function(dec, pos_char, neg_char) {\n    hemi <- if (dec >= 0) pos_char else neg_char\n    dec  <- abs(dec)\n    d    <- floor(dec)\n    m    <- floor((dec - d) * 60)\n    s    <- round(((dec - d) * 60 - m) * 60)\n    if (s == 60) { m <- m + 1; s <- 0 }\n    if (m == 60) { d <- d + 1; m <- 0 }\n    paste(d, m, s, hemi, sep = \"|\")\n  }\n  lat_str <- dec_to_dms(lat, \"N\", \"S\")\n  lon_str <- dec_to_dms(lon, \"E\", \"W\")\n  sprintf(\"{{coord|%s|%s|region:BO|display=inline,title}}\", lat_str, lon_str)\n}\n\n# ── Top ethnicities ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Returns a wikitext string of ethnic groups declared by ≥ threshold of pop.\n# Uses Wikipedia article names where a standard link exists.\nethnicity_wikilinks <- c(\n  \"Quechua\"      = \"[[Quechua people|Quechua]]\",\n  \"Aymara\"       = \"[[Aymara people|Aymara]]\",\n  \"Chiquitano\"   = \"[[Chiquitano people|Chiquitano]]\",\n  \"Guaran\\u00ed\" = \"[[Guaran\\u00ed people|Guaran\\u00ed]]\",\n  \"Moje\\u00f1o\"  = \"[[Moxo people|Moje\\u00f1o]]\",\n  \"Afroboliviano\" = \"[[Afro-Bolivians|Afroboliviano]]\",\n  \"Uru\"          = \"[[Uru people|Uru]]\",\n  \"Chipaya\"      = \"[[Chipaya people|Chipaya]]\",\n  \"Yuracar\\u00e9\" = \"[[Yuqui people|Yuracar\\u00e9]]\",\n  \"Movima\"       = \"[[Movima people|Movima]]\",\n  \"Tacana\"       = \"[[Tacana people|Tacana]]\",\n  \"Guaran\\u00ed\" = \"[[Guaran\\u00ed people|Guaran\\u00ed]]\",\n  \"Weenhayek\"    = \"[[Weenhayek people|Weenhayek]]\",\n  \"Tsimane\"      = \"[[Tsimane|Tsimane]]\",\n  \"Ayoreo\"       = \"[[Ayoreo people|Ayoreo]]\",\n  \"Ese Ejja\"     = \"[[Ese Ejja people|Ese Ejja]]\",\n  \"Leco\"         = \"[[Leco people|Leco]]\",\n  \"Kallawaya\"    = \"[[Kallawaya people|Kallawaya]]\",\n  \"Moseten\"      = \"[[Moset\\u00e9n people|Moseten]]\"\n)\n\nget_top_ethnicities <- function(id, auto_data, pop_total, threshold = 0.01) {\n  row <- auto_data |> filter(ine_code == id) |> slice(1)\n  if (nrow(row) == 0) return(\"\")\n\n  meta_cols <- c(\"ine_code\", \"municipio\", \"provincia\", \"departamento\",\n                 \"area\", \"nivel\", \"total\", \"municipality\", \"province\",\n                 \"department\", \"level\", \"row_num\")\n  grp_cols <- setdiff(names(row), meta_cols)\n\n  groups <- tibble(\n    group = grp_cols,\n    n     = sapply(grp_cols, function(col) as.double(row[[col]]))\n  ) |>\n    filter(n / pop_total >= threshold) |>\n    arrange(desc(n))\n\n  if (nrow(groups) == 0) return(\"\")\n\n  # Use wikilinks where available, otherwise plain name\n  linked <- sapply(groups$group, function(g) {\n    if (g %in% names(ethnicity_wikilinks)) ethnicity_wikilinks[[g]] else g\n  }, USE.NAMES = FALSE)\n\n  paste(linked, collapse = \", \")\n}\n\n# ── Infobox builder ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nbuild_infobox <- function(id, muni_lookup, prov_lookup, pop_muni, pop_muni_ur,\n                          area_muni, alcaldes, auto_data) {\n  ml <- muni_lookup |> filter(id_muni == id) |> slice(1)\n  if (nrow(ml) == 0) return(NULL)\n\n  muni_name  <- ml$muni_anexo\n  department <- ml$department\n  id_prov    <- ml$id_prov\n\n  # Province name (use cpv2024 name if available, fall back to anexo)\n  prov_row <- prov_lookup |> filter(id_prov == .env$id_prov) |> slice(1)\n  prov_name <- if (nrow(prov_row) > 0) {\n    coalesce(prov_row$province_cpv2024, prov_row$province_anexo)\n  } else NA_character_\n\n  # Population\n  pop_row <- pop_muni |> filter(id_muni == id) |> slice(1)\n  pop_total <- if (nrow(pop_row) > 0) pop_row$pop_2024 else NA_real_\n\n  urban_pop <- pop_muni_ur |> filter(id_muni == id, area == \"Urbana\") |>\n    pull(pop_2024) |> (\\(x) if (length(x) == 0) NA_real_ else x)()\n  rural_pop <- pop_muni_ur |> filter(id_muni == id, area == \"Rural\") |>\n    pull(pop_2024) |> (\\(x) if (length(x) == 0) NA_real_ else x)()\n\n  # Area and coordinates\n  geo <- area_muni |> filter(id_muni == id) |> slice(1)\n  area_km2   <- if (nrow(geo) > 0) round(geo$area_sqkm) else \"\"\n  coord_str  <- if (nrow(geo) > 0 && !is.na(geo$center_lat)) {\n    fmt_coord(geo$center_lat, geo$center_lon)\n  } else \"\"\n\n  # Population density\n  density_km2 <- if (!is.na(pop_total) && nrow(geo) > 0 && !is.na(geo$area_sqkm) && geo$area_sqkm > 0) {\n    round(pop_total / geo$area_sqkm)\n  } else \"\"\n\n  # Mayor\n  alc <- alcaldes |> filter(id_muni == id) |> slice(1)\n  mayor_str <- if (nrow(alc) > 0) {\n    paste0(alc$nombre, \" (\", alc$sigla, \", 2026)\")\n  } else \"\"\n\n  # Ethnicities\n  eth_str <- if (!is.na(pop_total)) {\n    get_top_ethnicities(id, auto_data, pop_total)\n  } else \"\"\n\n  # Build department and province wikilinks\n  dept_link <- sprintf(\"[[%s Department|%s]]\", department, department)\n  prov_link <- if (!is.na(prov_name)) {\n    sprintf(\"[[%s Province|%s]]\", prov_name, prov_name)\n  } else \"\"\n\n  # Pushpin map caption\n  pushpin_caption <- sprintf(\"Location of %s Municipality within Bolivia\", muni_name)\n\n  # Format numbers with commas\n  fn <- function(x) if (is.na(x) || x == \"\") \"\" else format(as.numeric(x), big.mark = \",\", scientific = FALSE)\n\n  paste0(\n'{{Infobox settlement\n<!--See the Table at Infobox Settlement for all fields and descriptions of usage-->\n<!-- Basic info  ---------------->\n|official_name          = ', muni_name, '\n|other_name             =\n|native_name            =\n|nickname               =\n|settlement_type        = Municipality\n|motto                  =\n<!-- images and maps  ----------->\n|image_skyline          =\n|imagesize              =\n|image_caption          =\n|image_flag             =\n|flag_size              =\n|image_seal             =\n|seal_size              =\n|image_shield           =\n|shield_size            =\n|image_blank_emblem     =\n|blank_emblem_type      =\n|blank_emblem_size      =\n|image_map              =\n|mapsize                =\n|map_caption            =\n|image_map1             =\n|mapsize1               =\n|map_caption1           =\n|image_dot_map          =\n|pushpin_map            = Bolivia\n|pushpin_label_position = bottom\n|pushpin_map_caption    = ', pushpin_caption, '\n<!-- Location ------------------>\n|subdivision_type       = Country\n|subdivision_name       = [[Image:Flag of Bolivia.svg|25px]] [[Bolivia]]\n|subdivision_type1      = [[Departments of Bolivia|Department]]\n|subdivision_name1      = ', dept_link, '\n|subdivision_type2      = [[Provinces of Bolivia|Province]]\n|subdivision_name2      = ', prov_link, '\n|subdivision_type4      = [[Cantons of Bolivia|Cantons]]\n|subdivision_name4      =\n<!-- Smaller parts (e.g. boroughs of a city) and seat of government -->\n|seat_type              = Seat\n|seat                   =\n<!-- Politics ----------------->\n|government_footnotes   =\n|government_type        =\n|leader_title           = Mayor\n|leader_name            = ', mayor_str, '\n|leader_title1          =\n|leader_name1           =\n|established_title      =\n|established_date       =\n<!-- Area    --------------------->\n|area_magnitude         =\n|unit_pref              = Metric\n|area_footnotes         =\n|area_total_km2         = ', fn(area_km2), '\n|area_land_km2          =\n|area_water_km2         =\n<!-- Population   ----------------------->\n|population_as_of               = 2024\n|population_footnotes           =\n|population_note                = [[2024 Bolivian census]]\n|population_total               = ', fn(pop_total), '\n|population_density_km2         = ', fn(density_km2), '\n|population_urban               = ', fn(urban_pop), '\n|population_blank1_title        = Ethnicities\n|population_blank1              = ', eth_str, '\n<!-- General information  --------------->\n|timezone               = [[Time in Bolivia|BOT]]\n|utc_offset             = -4\n|coordinates            = ', coord_str, '\n|elevation_footnotes    =\n|elevation_m            =\n<!-- Area/postal codes & others -------->\n|postal_code_type       =\n|postal_code            =\n|area_code              =\n|website                =\n|footnotes              =\n}}')\n}\n\n# ── Wikitext copy block ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nwikitext_block <- function(id, label, content) {\n  tagList(\n    tags$button(\n      class = \"copy-btn\",\n      style = \"margin-bottom: 6px;\",\n      onclick = sprintf(\"copyTableToClipboard('%s', this)\", id),\n      label\n    ),\n    tags$pre(\n      tags$code(content),\n      style = paste(\n        \"background-color: #f6f8fa; padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px;\",\n        \"overflow-x: auto; font-size: 11px; white-space: pre; margin: 0;\"\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$textarea(\n      id = id,\n      style = \"position: absolute; left: -9999px; height: 0; width: 0; overflow: hidden;\",\n      readonly = TRUE,\n      content\n    )\n  )\n}\n\n# ── Municipality block ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nmuni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, infobox_wikitext) {\n  tagList(\n    tags$details(\n      tags$summary(\n        tags$strong(muni_name),\n        tags$span(\n          paste0(\" \\u2014 \", department),\n          style = \"color: #586069; font-weight: normal;\"\n        ),\n        tags$code(\n          paste0(\" [\", id_muni, \"]\"),\n          style = \"font-size: 11px; color: #999; margin-left: 6px;\"\n        )\n      ),\n      tags$div(\n        style = \"margin-top: 8px; font-size: 12px;\",\n        wikitext_block(\n          id      = paste0(\"infobox_\", id_muni),\n          label   = \"Copy infobox\",\n          content = infobox_wikitext\n        )\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$hr(style = \"border:none; border-top:1px solid #e1e4e8; margin:4px 0;\")\n  )\n}\n\n# ── Render all blocks ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup) {\n  ids <- lookup |>\n    arrange(id_muni) |>\n    select(id_muni, muni_anexo, department)\n\n  tagList(pmap(ids, function(id_muni, muni_anexo, department) {\n    ib <- build_infobox(id_muni, muni_lookup, prov_lookup, pop_muni, pop_muni_ur,\n                        area_muni, alcaldes, auto_data)\n    if (is.null(ib)) return(NULL)\n    muni_block(id_muni, muni_anexo, department, ib)\n  }))\n}\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: filter-summary\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nn_total <- nrow(muni_lookup)\nfilter_label <- switch(params$wiki_filter,\n  existing = \"with an existing English Wikipedia article\",\n  new      = \"without an English Wikipedia article (new)\",\n  \"all (no filter applied)\"\n)\ncat(sprintf(\"**Rendering infoboxes for %d municipalities %s.**\\n\\n\", n_total, filter_label))\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: infobox-blocks\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_muni_blocks(muni_lookup)\n```\n","language":"markdown"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01HsNBjXrMdfxhTDpbVrjnvs"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(dplyr)\n\nmuni_lookup_t <- readRDS(\"data/muni_id_lookup_table.rds\") |>\n  mutate(id_p...[truncated, total 2655 chars]","intent":"Load test data for infobox function"},"intent":"Load test data for infobox function","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(dplyr)\n\nmuni_lookup_t <- readRDS(\"data/muni_id_lookup_table.rds\") |>\n  mutate(id_prov = stringr::str_sub(id_muni, 1, 4))\nprov_lookup  <- readRDS(\"data/prov_id_lookup_table.rds\")\nalcaldes     <- readRDS(\"data/oep2026/alcaldes.rds\")\n\npop_muni <- readRDS(\"data/cpv2024/population_municipality.rds\") |>\n  select(id_muni = ine_code, municipality, department, province,\n         pop_2024 = pop_2024_total, pop_2012 = pop_2012_total,\n         pop_2001 = pop_2001_total)\n\npop_muni_ur <- readRDS(\"data/cpv2024/population_municipality_urb_rural.rds\") |>\n  select(id_muni = ine_code, area, pop_2024 = pop_2024_total)\n\nauto_data <- readRDS(\"data/cpv2024/autoidentificacion_municipio.rds\")\n\narea_muni <- readxl::read_excel(\"maps/bol_admin_boundaries.xlsx\", sheet = \"bol_admin3\") |>\n  mutate(id_muni = stringr::str_sub(adm3_pcode, 3, 8)) |>\n  select(id_muni, area_sqkm, center_lat, center_lon)\n\n# Source the helper functions by running the code inline\nfmt_coord <- function(lat, lon) {\n  dec_to_dms <- function(dec, pos_char, neg_char) {\n    hemi <- if (dec >= 0) pos_char else neg_char\n    dec  <- abs(dec)\n    d    <- floor(dec)\n    m    <- floor((dec - d) * 60)\n    s    <- round(((dec - d) * 60 - m) * 60)\n    if (s == 60) { m <- m + 1; s <- 0 }\n    if (m == 60) { d <- d + 1; m <- 0 }\n    paste(d, m, s, hemi, sep = \"|\")\n  }\n  lat_str <- dec_to_dms(lat, \"N\", \"S\")\n  lon_str <- dec_to_dms(lon, \"E\", \"W\")\n  sprintf(\"{{coord|%s|%s|region:BO|display=inline,title}}\", lat_str, lon_str)\n}\n\nethnicity_wikilinks <- c(\n  \"Quechua\"      = \"[[Quechua people|Quechua]]\",\n  \"Aymara\"       = \"[[Aymara people|Aymara]]\",\n  \"Chiquitano\"   = \"[[Chiquitano people|Chiquitano]]\",\n  \"Guaran\\u00ed\" = \"[[Guaran\\u00ed people|Guaran\\u00ed]]\",\n  \"Moje\\u00f1o\"  = \"[[Moxo people|Moje\\u00f1o]]\",\n  \"Afroboliviano\" = \"[[Afro-Bolivians|Afroboliviano]]\"\n)\n\nget_top_ethnicities <- function(id, auto_data, pop_total, threshold = 0.01) {\n  row <- auto_data |> filter(ine_code == id) |> slice(1)\n  if (nrow(row) == 0) return(\"\")\n  meta_cols <- c(\"ine_code\", \"municipio\", \"provincia\", \"departamento\",\n                 \"area\", \"nivel\", \"total\", \"municipality\", \"province\",\n                 \"department\", \"level\", \"row_num\")\n  grp_cols <- setdiff(names(row), meta_cols)\n  groups <- tibble(group = grp_cols,\n                   n = sapply(grp_cols, function(col) as.double(row[[col]]))) |>\n    filter(n / pop_total >= threshold) |>\n    arrange(desc(n))\n  if (nrow(groups) == 0) return(\"\")\n  linked <- sapply(groups$group, function(g) {\n    if (g %in% names(ethnicity_wikilinks)) ethnicity_wikilinks[[g]] else g\n  }, USE.NAMES = FALSE)\n  paste(linked, collapse = \", \")\n}\n\ncat(\"Setup complete\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Both fixes work. Here's a summary of [bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd](bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd):

**Data sources used** (all already loaded in the project):

| Field | Source |
|-------|--------|
| Name, department, province | `muni_id_lookup_table.rds` + `prov_id_lookup_table.rds` |
| Population (total, urban) | `population_municipality.rds` + `…_urb_rural.rds` |
| Area + centroid coordinates | `bol_admin_boundaries.xlsx` |
| Mayor | `alcaldes.rds` (title-cased from ALL CAPS source) |
| Ethnicities (≥1% of pop) | `autoidentificacion_municipio.rds` |
| Constants | `settlement_type=Municipality`, `timezone=BOT`, `utc_offset=-4` |

**Features**: `wiki_filter` parameter (same as the other generator), copy buttons, footnote markers stripped from ethnicity names, wikilinks for major ethnic groups.

**Left blank** for manual fill: `native_name`, `image_*`, `seat`, `cantons`, `elevation_m`, `established_*`, `area_code`.


<span class="suggestion">Add the `{{Population pyramid 2}}` template as a second copyable block alongside each infobox.</span>


<span class="suggestion">Expand the `ethnicity_wikilinks` map to cover more groups like Campesino, Jalq'a, etc.</span>


<span class="suggestion">Render with `wiki_filter = "new"` to generate infoboxes only for the 148 municipalities without articles.</span>





## User

Model the map section of the infobox on this:

|image_map              = Tarabuco muni locator map.svg
|mapsize                = 300px
|map_caption            = Location of the Tarabuco Municipality within Bolivia
|image_map1             = {{maplink |type=shape | stroke-width = 3 | fill = #999999 |frame=yes |plain=yes |frame-align=center |zoom=8}}

The appropriate name appears in commons_upload_log.csv with the filename in the column commons_filename.

No custom code is required for image_map1, since it reads the OpenStreetMap object from Wikidata, but we should consult Wikidata and make sure that P402 is part of the corresponding item before including it here.

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_0155jfhQB2tdMqV7z3Ahgo9U"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"R/get_wikidata_instances.R:222:# ---- .build_sparql_query --------------------------------...[truncated, total 2167 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"R/get_wikidata_instances.R:222:# ---- .build_sparql_query ------------------------------------------------\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:223:# Internal helper: build a SPARQL query to retrieve items that are instances\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:226:.build_sparql_query <- function(class_qid, country = NULL, property_id = \"P31\", limit = 1000) {\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:438:# ---- .sparql_get_qids --------------------------------------------------------\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:439:# Internal helper: run the SPARQL query and return a character vector of QIDs.\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:441:.sparql_get_qids <- function(class_qid, country, limit, property_id = \"P31\") {\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:442:sparql_query <- .build_sparql_query(class_qid, country, property_id, limit)\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:445:url   = \"https://query.wikidata.org/sparql\",\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:446:query = list(query = sparql_query, format = \"json\"),\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:451:stop(\"SPARQL query failed with status: \", status_code(response))\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:600:# Step 1: SPARQL — get all QIDs\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:601:qids <- .sparql_get_qids(class_qid, country, limit, property_id)\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:628:#' Resume a Partially-Completed get_wikidata_instances() Query\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:631:#' through and you have a partial result. Re-runs the SPARQL query to obtain\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:725:# Step 1: re-run SPARQL to get the complete QID list\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:726:message(\"Re-running SPARQL query for \", class_qid, \"...\")\nR/get_wikidata_instances.R:727:all_qids <- .sparql_get_qids(class_qid, country, limit, property_id)\nR/get_wikidata_list.R:185:# Step 1: SPARQL — get all QIDs\nR/get_wikidata_list.R:186:qids <- .sparql_get_qids(class_qid, country, limit, property_id)\nR/get_wikidata_list.R:213:#' Resume a Partially-Completed get_wikidata_instances() Query\nR/get_wikidata_list.R:249:# Step 1: re-run SPARQL to get the complete QID list\nR/get_wikidata_list.R:250:message(\"Re-running SPARQL query for \", class_qid, \"...\")\nR/get_wikidata_list.R:251:all_qids <- .sparql_get_qids(class_qid, country, limit, property_id)\n","language":"text"},"elapsedMs":740}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01C96V8hrAYqcqxXvxkJTrzo"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# Query Wikidata for Bolivian municipalities with P402 (OSM relation ID)\nlibrary(httr)\nlib...[truncated, total 597 chars]","intent":"Query Wikidata for municipalities with OSM relation IDs"},"intent":"Query Wikidata for municipalities with OSM relation IDs","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# Query Wikidata for Bolivian municipalities with P402 (OSM relation ID)\nlibrary(httr)\nlibrary(jsonlite)\n\nsparql <- '\nSELECT ?item ?osm WHERE {\n  ?item wdt:P31 wd:Q2555896 ;  \n        wdt:P17 wd:Q750 ;\n        wdt:P402 ?osm .\n}\n'\n\nresp <- GET(\n  \"https://query.wikidata.org/sparql\",\n  query = list(query = sparql, format = \"json\"),\n  add_headers(`User-Agent` = \"wiki-graph-bot/1.0\")\n)\n\nresults <- content(resp, as = \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\") |> fromJSON()\np402_qids <- results$results$bindings$item$value |>\n  basename()\n\ncat(\"Municipalities with P402:\", length(p402_qids), \"\\n\")\nhead(p402_qids)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_014nwLfd8m9NdDc2iCw7pmS6"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 13.5KB, 388 lines, showing lines 38-67 (truncated)]\n    38→\n    39→pop_muni_ur <- r...[truncated, total 1376 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 13.5KB, 388 lines, showing lines 38-67 (truncated)]\n\npop_muni_ur <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"population_municipality_urb_rural.rds\")) |>\n  select(id_muni = ine_code, area, pop_2024 = pop_2024_total)\n\n# Ethnic identification\nauto_data <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"autoidentificacion_municipio.rds\"))\n\n# Municipality areas + centroid coordinates\narea_muni <- readxl::read_excel(here::here(\"maps\", \"bol_admin_boundaries.xlsx\"),\n                                sheet = \"bol_admin3\") |>\n  mutate(id_muni = stringr::str_sub(adm3_pcode, 3, 8)) |>\n  select(id_muni, area_sqkm, center_lat, center_lon)\n\n# Wikipedia presence filter\nwiki_presence <- bo_municipality_wiki_presence$data |>\n  left_join(\n    readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"municipalities_wd_ine.rds\")) |>\n      select(qid, ine_code),\n    by = \"qid\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(!is.na(ine_code))\n\nmunis_with_enwiki <- wiki_presence |> filter(en > 0) |> pull(ine_code)\n\nif (params$wiki_filter == \"existing\") {\n  muni_lookup <- muni_lookup |> filter(id_muni %in% munis_with_enwiki)\n} else if (params$wiki_filter == \"new\") {\n  muni_lookup <- muni_lookup |> filter(!id_muni %in% munis_with_enwiki)\n}\n"},"elapsedMs":96}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01GrDhxqRbXrQyvsT2LyU1nQ"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","old_string":"# Wikipedia presence filter\nwiki_presence <- bo_municipality_wiki_presence$data |>\n  left_...[truncated, total 309 chars]","new_string":"# Locator map filenames from Commons upload log\ncommons_log <- readr::read_csv(\n  here::he...[truncated, total 1175 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -48,13 +48,36 @@\n   mutate(id_muni = stringr::str_sub(adm3_pcode, 3, 8)) |>\n   select(id_muni, area_sqkm, center_lat, center_lon)\n \n+# Locator map filenames from Commons upload log\n+commons_log <- readr::read_csv(\n+  here::here(\"output\", \"locator_maps\", \"muni-maps-final\", \"commons_upload_log.csv\"),\n+  show_col_types = FALSE\n+) |>\n+  select(id_muni, commons_filename) |>\n+  filter(!is.na(commons_filename))\n+\n+# Wikidata QID ↔ INE crosswalk (reused for multiple lookups)\n+wd_ine <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"municipalities_wd_ine.rds\")) |>\n+  select(qid, ine_code)\n+\n+# P402 (OSM relation ID) presence — query Wikidata\n+p402_qids <- {\n+  resp <- httr::GET(\n+    \"https://query.wikidata.org/sparql\",\n+    query = list(\n+      query = \"SELECT ?item WHERE { ?item wdt:P31 wd:Q1062710 ; wdt:P402 ?osm . }\",\n+      format = \"json\"\n+    ),\n+    httr::add_headers(`User-Agent` = \"wiki-graph-bot/1.0\")\n+  )\n+  results <- jsonlite::fromJSON(httr::content(resp, as = \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"))\n+  basename(results$results$bindings$item$value)\n+}\n+munis_with_p402 <- wd_ine |> filter(qid %in% p402_qids) |> pull(ine_code)\n+\n # Wikipedia presence filter\n wiki_presence <- bo_municipality_wiki_presence$data |>\n-  left_join(\n-    readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"municipalities_wd_ine.rds\")) |>\n-      select(qid, ine_code),\n-    by = \"qid\"\n-  ) |>\n+  left_join(wd_ine, by = \"qid\") |>\n   filter(!is.na(ine_code))\n \n munis_with_enwiki <- wiki_presence |> filter(en > 0) |> pull(ine_code)\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01AJeW7mSitwZ81t36gK7QVC"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 14.4KB, 411 lines, showing lines 130-159 (truncated)]\n   130→  \"Yuracar\\u00e9\" = \"[...[truncated, total 1445 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 14.4KB, 411 lines, showing lines 130-159 (truncated)]\n  \"Yuracar\\u00e9\" = \"[[Yuqui people|Yuracar\\u00e9]]\",\n  \"Movima\"       = \"[[Movima people|Movima]]\",\n  \"Tacana\"       = \"[[Tacana people|Tacana]]\",\n  \"Guaran\\u00ed\" = \"[[Guaran\\u00ed people|Guaran\\u00ed]]\",\n  \"Weenhayek\"    = \"[[Weenhayek people|Weenhayek]]\",\n  \"Tsimane\"      = \"[[Tsimane|Tsimane]]\",\n  \"Ayoreo\"       = \"[[Ayoreo people|Ayoreo]]\",\n  \"Ese Ejja\"     = \"[[Ese Ejja people|Ese Ejja]]\",\n  \"Leco\"         = \"[[Leco people|Leco]]\",\n  \"Kallawaya\"    = \"[[Kallawaya people|Kallawaya]]\",\n  \"Moseten\"      = \"[[Moset\\u00e9n people|Moseten]]\"\n)\n\nget_top_ethnicities <- function(id, auto_data, pop_total, threshold = 0.01) {\n  row <- auto_data |> filter(ine_code == id) |> slice(1)\n  if (nrow(row) == 0) return(\"\")\n\n  meta_cols <- c(\"ine_code\", \"municipio\", \"provincia\", \"departamento\",\n                 \"area\", \"nivel\", \"total\", \"municipality\", \"province\",\n                 \"department\", \"level\", \"row_num\")\n  grp_cols <- setdiff(names(row), meta_cols)\n\n  groups <- tibble(\n    group = grp_cols,\n    n     = sapply(grp_cols, function(col) as.double(row[[col]]))\n  ) |>\n    filter(n / pop_total >= threshold) |>\n    arrange(desc(n))\n\n  if (nrow(groups) == 0) return(\"\")"},"elapsedMs":57}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_0178hXD1vtEbyrfc33kEmFQL"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","old_string":"  # Pushpin map caption\n  pushpin_caption <- sprintf(\"Location of %s Municipality within B...[truncated, total 252 chars]","new_string":"  # Pushpin map caption\n  pushpin_caption <- sprintf(\"Location of %s Municipality within B...[truncated, total 778 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -227,6 +227,18 @@\n   # Pushpin map caption\n   pushpin_caption <- sprintf(\"Location of %s Municipality within Bolivia\", muni_name)\n \n+  # Locator map from Commons upload log\n+  map_row <- commons_log |> filter(id_muni == id) |> slice(1)\n+  map_file    <- if (nrow(map_row) > 0) map_row$commons_filename else \"\"\n+  map_caption <- if (nzchar(map_file)) {\n+    sprintf(\"Location of the %s Municipality within Bolivia\", muni_name)\n+  } else \"\"\n+\n+  # OSM maplink: only include if Wikidata item has P402\n+  map1_str <- if (id %in% munis_with_p402) {\n+    \"{{maplink |type=shape |stroke-width=3 |fill=#999999 |frame=yes |plain=yes |frame-align=center |zoom=8}}\"\n+  } else \"\"\n+\n   # Format numbers with commas\n   fn <- function(x) if (is.na(x) || x == \"\") \"\" else format(as.numeric(x), big.mark = \",\", scientific = FALSE)\n \n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_015StV3epBeEZ7bUXEyhkHrW"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 15.0KB, 424 lines, showing lines 383-402 (truncated)]\n   383→# ── Render all blocks...[truncated, total 789 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 15.0KB, 424 lines, showing lines 383-402 (truncated)]\n# ── Render all blocks ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup) {\n  ids <- lookup |>\n    arrange(id_muni) |>\n    select(id_muni, muni_anexo, department)\n\n  tagList(pmap(ids, function(id_muni, muni_anexo, department) {\n    ib <- build_infobox(id_muni, muni_lookup, prov_lookup, pop_muni, pop_muni_ur,\n                        area_muni, alcaldes, auto_data)\n    if (is.null(ib)) return(NULL)\n    muni_block(id_muni, muni_anexo, department, ib)\n  }))\n}\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()"},"elapsedMs":86}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01YT9sLmA3RRws1oRyLQQzD3"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 31.4KB, 791 lines, showing lines 1-80 (truncated)]\n     1→# ---- add_wikidata_prope...[truncated, total 3140 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 31.4KB, 791 lines, showing lines 1-80 (truncated)]\n# ---- add_wikidata_property --------------------------------------------------\n\n#' Add a Wikidata Property to a Data Frame\n#'\n#' Fetches a single-valued property from Wikidata and appends it as a new\n#' column to a data frame that contains a `qid` column. Handles entity-type,\n#' string, and time values. Issues a message when an item has multiple\n#' statements for the requested property.\n#'\n#' @param df A data frame with a `qid` column.\n#' @param property Character. Wikidata property ID (e.g., \"P14142\").\n#' @param name Character. Name of the new column. Defaults to the property ID.\n#'\n#' @return The input data frame with a new character column appended.\n#'\n#' @examples\n#' departments |> add_wikidata_property(\"P14142\", name = \"ine_code\")\nadd_wikidata_property <- function(df, property, name = property) {\n\n  if (!\"qid\" %in% names(df)) stop(\"df must contain a 'qid' column\")\n  if (!grepl(\"^P\\\\d+$\", property)) stop(\"property must be in format 'P123'\")\n\n  qids <- df$qid\n\n  values <- map_chr(qids, function(qid) {\n    Sys.sleep(0.1)\n\n    tryCatch({\n      r <- GET(\n        \"https://www.wikidata.org/w/api.php\",\n        query = list(\n          action = \"wbgetentities\",\n          ids = qid,\n          format = \"json\",\n          props = \"claims\"\n        )\n      )\n\n      entity <- fromJSON(content(r, \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"))$entities[[qid]]\n      claims <- entity$claims[[property]]\n\n      if (is.null(claims) || nrow(claims) == 0) {\n        return(NA_character_)\n      }\n\n      if (nrow(claims) > 1) {\n        message(qid, \" has \", nrow(claims), \" values for \", property,\n                \"; using the first (rank: \", claims$rank[1], \")\")\n      }\n\n      snak <- claims$mainsnak[1, ]\n      dv   <- snak$datavalue[[1]]\n\n      # Dispatch on value type\n      if (is.data.frame(dv) && \"amount\" %in% names(dv)) {\n        sub(\"^\\\\+\", \"\", dv$amount[[1]])\n      } else if (is.data.frame(dv)) {\n        as.character(dv$id)\n      } else if (is.character(dv)) {\n        dv\n      } else {\n        as.character(dv)\n      }\n\n    }, error = function(e) {\n      message(\"Error on \", qid, \": \", conditionMessage(e))\n      NA_character_\n    })\n  })\n\n  df[[name]] <- values\n  df\n}\n\n# ---- .extract_numeric_list_property -----------------------------------------\n# Internal helper: extract all claims for a single quantity property from one\n# entity, including per-claim year (P585 qualifier) and reference (P854 URL\n# or P248 \"stated in\" QID). Returns a flat named list of scalars suitable for\n# inclusion in a bind_rows() record.\n#"},"elapsedMs":124}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01X4sRUYLdkTPFpUU8DLPSHV"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"read","input":{"file_path":"R/get_wikidata_instances.R","offset":533,"limit":50},"caption":"R/get_wikidata_instances.R:533-582","captionLink":"R/get_wikidata_instances.R:533"}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01X4sRUYLdkTPFpUU8DLPSHV"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 31.4KB, 791 lines, showing lines 533-582 (truncated)]\n   533→get_wikidata_instances...[truncated, total 2830 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 31.4KB, 791 lines, showing lines 533-582 (truncated)]\nget_wikidata_instances <- function(class_qid,\n                                   property                    = NULL,\n                                   property_names              = NULL,\n                                   country                     = NULL,\n                                   languages                   = c(\"en\", \"es\"),\n                                   limit                       = 1000,\n                                   batch_size                  = 50,\n                                   batch_delay                 = 1,\n                                   numeric_list_properties     = NULL,\n                                   numeric_list_property_names = NULL,\n                                   entity_props                = \"labels|descriptions|claims|sitelinks|aliases\",\n                                   object_type                 = \"instance\") {\n\n  # Validate object_type\n  if (!object_type %in% c(\"instance\", \"subclass\")) {\n    stop('object_type must be \"instance\" or \"subclass\"')\n  }\n\n  # Resolve column names for regular extra properties\n  if (!is.null(property)) {\n    n_prop  <- length(property)\n    n_names <- length(property_names)\n    if (n_names > n_prop) {\n      message(\"property_names has more entries (\", n_names, \") than property (\",\n              n_prop, \"); extra names will be ignored.\")\n      property_names <- property_names[seq_len(n_prop)]\n    } else if (n_names < n_prop) {\n      if (n_names > 0)\n        message(\"property_names has fewer entries (\", n_names, \") than property (\",\n                n_prop, \"); falling back to property IDs for unnamed columns.\")\n      property_names <- c(property_names, property[(n_names + 1):n_prop])\n    }\n  }\n\n  # Resolve column name prefixes for numeric list properties\n  numeric_list_properties <- as.character(numeric_list_properties)\n  if (length(numeric_list_properties) > 0) {\n    if (is.null(numeric_list_property_names))\n      numeric_list_property_names <- numeric_list_properties\n    n_nlp  <- length(numeric_list_properties)\n    n_nlpn <- length(numeric_list_property_names)\n    if (n_nlpn < n_nlp)\n      numeric_list_property_names <- c(numeric_list_property_names,\n                                       numeric_list_properties[(n_nlpn + 1):n_nlp])\n    if (n_nlpn > n_nlp)\n      numeric_list_property_names <- numeric_list_property_names[seq_len(n_nlp)]\n  }\n\n  # Validate input\n  if (!grepl(\"^Q\\\\d+$\", class_qid))"},"elapsedMs":93}</script></tool-result></div>

269 of 340 have P402 — consistent with the earlier SPARQL result. Now let me update the infobox QMD to read from this file instead of querying Wikidata.

<div><tool-call id="toolu_011cKyxWXihi6Y6FiNXUpphF"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","old_string":"# Wikidata QID ↔ INE crosswalk (reused for multiple lookups)\nwd_ine <- readRDS(here::here(...[truncated, total 687 chars]","new_string":"# Wikidata QID ↔ INE crosswalk (reused for multiple lookups)\nwd_ine <- readRDS(here::here(...[truncated, total 670 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -60,20 +60,17 @@\n wd_ine <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"municipalities_wd_ine.rds\")) |>\n   select(qid, ine_code)\n \n-# P402 (OSM relation ID) presence — query Wikidata\n-p402_qids <- {\n-  resp <- httr::GET(\n-    \"https://query.wikidata.org/sparql\",\n-    query = list(\n-      query = \"SELECT ?item WHERE { ?item wdt:P31 wd:Q1062710 ; wdt:P402 ?osm . }\",\n-      format = \"json\"\n-    ),\n-    httr::add_headers(`User-Agent` = \"wiki-graph-bot/1.0\")\n-  )\n-  results <- jsonlite::fromJSON(httr::content(resp, as = \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"))\n-  basename(results$results$bindings$item$value)\n-}\n-munis_with_p402 <- wd_ine |> filter(qid %in% p402_qids) |> pull(ine_code)\n+# P402 (OSM relation ID) presence — from saved Wikidata snapshot\n+# To refresh: source(\"R/get_wikidata_instances.R\"), then run:\n+#   get_wikidata_instances(\"Q1062710\", property = \"P402\",\n+#     property_names = \"osm_relation_id\", country = \"Q750\") |>\n+#     saveRDS(\"data/municipalities_wd_p402.rds\")\n+wd_p402 <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"municipalities_wd_p402.rds\"))\n+munis_with_p402 <- wd_p402 |>\n+  filter(!is.na(osm_relation_id)) |>\n+  left_join(wd_ine, by = \"qid\") |>\n+  filter(!is.na(ine_code)) |>\n+  pull(ine_code)\n \n # Wikipedia presence filter\n wiki_presence <- bo_municipality_wiki_presence$data |>\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01BVk2fDMA99WJYe9PXWDjo7"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# Define helpers inline for testing\nfmt_coord <- function(lat, lon) {\n  dec_to_dms <- func...[truncated, total 1752 chars]","intent":"Define helper functions for testing"},"intent":"Define helper functions for testing","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# Define helpers inline for testing\nfmt_coord <- function(lat, lon) {\n  dec_to_dms <- function(dec, pos_char, neg_char) {\n    hemi <- if (dec >= 0) pos_char else neg_char\n    dec  <- abs(dec)\n    d    <- floor(dec)\n    m    <- floor((dec - d) * 60)\n    s    <- round(((dec - d) * 60 - m) * 60)\n    if (s == 60) { m <- m + 1; s <- 0 }\n    if (m == 60) { d <- d + 1; m <- 0 }\n    paste(d, m, s, hemi, sep = \"|\")\n  }\n  sprintf(\"{{coord|%s|%s|region:BO|display=inline,title}}\",\n          dec_to_dms(lat, \"N\", \"S\"), dec_to_dms(lon, \"E\", \"W\"))\n}\n\nethnicity_wikilinks <- c(\n  \"Quechua\" = \"[[Quechua people|Quechua]]\",\n  \"Aymara\" = \"[[Aymara people|Aymara]]\",\n  \"Chiquitano\" = \"[[Chiquitano people|Chiquitano]]\",\n  \"Guaran\\u00ed\" = \"[[Guaran\\u00ed people|Guaran\\u00ed]]\",\n  \"Moje\\u00f1o\" = \"[[Moxo people|Moje\\u00f1o]]\",\n  \"Afroboliviano\" = \"[[Afro-Bolivians|Afroboliviano]]\"\n)\n\nget_top_ethnicities <- function(id, auto_data, pop_total, threshold = 0.01) {\n  row <- auto_data |> filter(ine_code == id) |> slice(1)\n  if (nrow(row) == 0) return(\"\")\n  meta_cols <- c(\"ine_code\", \"municipio\", \"provincia\", \"departamento\",\n                 \"area\", \"nivel\", \"total\", \"municipality\", \"province\",\n                 \"department\", \"level\", \"row_num\")\n  grp_cols <- setdiff(names(row), meta_cols)\n  groups <- tibble(group = grp_cols,\n                   n = sapply(grp_cols, function(col) as.double(row[[col]]))) |>\n    filter(n / pop_total >= threshold) |> arrange(desc(n))\n  if (nrow(groups) == 0) return(\"\")\n  linked <- sapply(groups$group, function(g) {\n    g_clean <- gsub(\"\\\\(\\\\d+\\\\)$\", \"\", trimws(g))\n    if (g %in% names(ethnicity_wikilinks)) ethnicity_wikilinks[[g]] else g_clean\n  }, USE.NAMES = FALSE)\n  paste(linked, collapse = \", \")\n}\n\ncat(\"Helpers defined\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

             commons_log, munis_with_p402) {\n  ml <- muni_lookup |> filter(id_muni == id) |> slice(1)\n  if (nrow(ml) == 0) return(NULL)\n  muni_name  <- ml$muni_anexo\n  department <- ml$department\n  id_prov    <- ml$id_prov\n\n  prov_row <- prov_lookup |> filter(id_prov == .env$id_prov) |> slice(1)\n  prov_name <- if (nrow(prov_row) > 0) coalesce(prov_row$province_cpv2024, prov_row$province_anexo) else NA_character_\n\n  pop_row <- pop_muni |> filter(id_muni == id) |> slice(1)\n  pop_total <- if (nrow(pop_row) > 0) pop_row$pop_2024 else NA_real_\n  urban_pop <- Everything works correctly. The locator map SVG is populated from the Commons log, and `image_map1` (the OSM maplink) is only included when the Wikidata item has P402.

The changes:

1. **Saved `municipalities_wd_p402.rds`** via a one-time `get_wikidata_instances()` call — 269 of 340 municipalities have P402.
2. **Updated [bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd](bolivia-muni-infobox-en.qmd)** to read from that file and from `commons_upload_log.csv`, with no runtime Wikidata queries. The setup block includes a comment showing how to refresh the P402 snapshot.
3. **Map fields** now populate:
   - `image_map` = SVG filename from Commons upload log (320 municipalities)
   - `map_caption` = "Location of the {name} Municipality within Bolivia"
   - `image_map1` = `{{maplink ...}}` template only when P402 exists on the Wikidata item (268 municipalities with INE codes)


<span class="suggestion">Render with `wiki_filter = "new"` to generate infoboxes for the 148 municipalities without Wikipedia articles.</span>


<span class="suggestion">Add P402 to the ~72 missing Wikidata items using QuickStatements with OSM relation IDs.</span>





## User

I looked at one example that doesn't have an OSM relation: San Buenaventura Municipality, Q1814898. Searching "Municipio San Buenaventura" in OSM finds: https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/4499584  Which then can be added back to Wikidata using a QuickStatement

See add_ine_codes_to_wikidata.R for R code making such statements using functions I've already written.

Use osmapiR or osmdata packages to automate this process for those Wikidata items not connected to OSM

Villazón (Modesto Omiste province)\n    .default = dept_final\n  ))\n\n\n# --- Round 1: Direct match on label_es + department --------------------------\nmatch_r1 <- wd_clean |>\n  inner_join(ine_geog_municipalities,\n             by = c(\"label_es\" = \"municipality\", \"dept_final\" = \"department\")) |>\n  select(qid, cod.mun)\n\n\n# --- Round 2: Cleaned names + department -------------------------------------\n# Strip common prefixes/suffixes that differ between WD and INE\nwd_clean <- wd_clean |>\n  mutate(\n    name_es_clean = label_es |>\n      str_remove(\"^Municipio( de)?\\\\s+\") |>\n      str_remove(\"\\\\s*\\\\(municipio\\\\)$\"),\n    name_en_clean = label_en |>\n      str_remove(\"\\\\s+[Mm]unicipality$\") |>\n      str_remove(\"^Municipio( de)?\\\\s+\"),\n    match_name = coalesce(name_es_clean, name_en_clean)\n  )\n\nunmatched_r1 <- wd_clean |> filter(!qid %in% match_r1$qid)\n\nmatch_r2 <- unmatched_r1 |>\n  inner_join(ine_geog_municipalities,\n             by = c(\"match_name\" = \"municipality\", \"dept_final\" = \"department\")) |>\n  select(qid, cod.mun)\n\n\n# --- Round 3: Manual matches -------------------------------------------------\n# Names that diverge enough between WD and INE to require explicit pairing.\nmatch_manual <- tribble(\n  ~qid,           ~cod.mun,\n  # Fuzzy name differences (accents, word order, abbreviations)\n  \"Q685208\",    \"080401\",  # Santa Ana del Yacuma    -> Santa Ana de Yacuma\n  \"Q721682\",    \"071501\",  # Ascencion de Guarayos   -> Ascension de Guarayos\n  \"Q647771\",    \"040503\",  # <span class="thinking-topic">Planning the OSM search approach</span>



Now I understand the pattern. I need to:
1. Identify which municipalities are missing P402
2. Search OSM for matching relations using the municipality name
3. Verify the matches
4. Generate QuickStatements to add P402

Let me check what `osmapiR` or `osmdata` can do for searching relations by name. The user mentioned searching "Municipio San Buenaventura" in OSM and finding relation 4499584.

The Overpass API can search for relations with specific tags. Bolivian municipalities in OSM typically have `boundary=administrative` and `admin_level=4` (or maybe 3 or 6 depending on the convention). Let me use `osmdata` to query for these.
I think the best approach is to query all administrative boundary relations in Bolivia and match them to our municipalities by name, rather than searching individually. I'll write an R script that pulls these relations from Overpass and handles the matching.
</thinking><span data-thinking-duration-ms="10552"></span></div>

## User

I looked at one example that doesn't have an OSM relation: San Buenaventura Municipality, Q1814898. Searching "Municipio San Buenaventura" in OSM finds: https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/4499584 Which then can be added back to Wikidata using a QuickStatement

See add_ine_codes_to_wikidata.R for R code making such statements using functions I've already written. See R/create_quick_statement.R

Use osmapiR or osmdata packages to automate this process for those Wikidata items not connected to OSM

Let's check what levels exist\n# Try admin_level 6 which is typical for municipalities in Latin America\ntest6 <- opq(bbox = c(-70, -23, -57, -9), timeout = 120) |>\n  add_osm_feature(key = \"boundary\", value = \"administrative\") |>\n  add_osm_feature(key = \"admin_level\", value = \"6\") |>\n  osmdata_sf()\n\ncat(\"Relations found at admin_level=6:\", nrow(test6$osm_multipolygons), \"\\n\")\nif (nrow(test6$osm_multipolygons) > 0) {\n  head(test6$osm_multipolygons |> st_drop_geometry() |> select(osm_id, name), 10)\n}","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><thinking id="thinking-1781889354063-l07nx">
Admin level 8 for municipalities. And importantly, some OSM relations already have the `wikidata` tag! That makes matching much easier. Let me query all admin_level=8 boundary relations in Bolivia.

Admin level 8 — and they have `wikidata` tags. Let me query all of them.

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01GHQfviSLCyr4mPAV1irKSn"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# Query all admin_level=8 boundary relations in Bolivia via Overpass\n# This is more reliab...[truncated, total 605 chars]","intent":"Query all Bolivian municipal boundary relations from OSM","timeout":120000},"intent":"Query all Bolivian municipal boundary relations from OSM","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# Query all admin_level=8 boundary relations in Bolivia via Overpass\n# This is more reliable than osmdata for getting all tags\noverpass_query <- '\n[out:json][timeout:180];\narea[\"ISO3166-1\"=\"BO\"]->.bolivia;\nrelation[\"boundary\"=\"administrative\"][\"admin_level\"=\"8\"](area.bolivia);\nout tags;\n'\n\nresp <- POST(\n  \"https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter\",\n  body = list(data = overpass_query),\n  encode = \"form\"\n)\n\nosm_result <- jsonlite::fromJSON(content(resp, \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"),\n                                  simplifyVector = FALSE)\ncat(\"OSM relations found:\", length(osm_result$elements), \"\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01GHQfviSLCyr4mPAV1irKSn"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"content","value":[{"type":"text","text":"Output:\nOSM relations found: 341 \n\n\nExecution time: 3248ms"}]},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"OSM relations found: 341"},"elapsedMs":3316}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01EjveNeQVWxhJ9DqcfU5JXv"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# Parse into a tidy data frame\nosm_munis <- purrr::map_dfr(osm_result$elements, function(e...[truncated, total 639 chars]","intent":"Parse OSM results into tidy dataframe"},"intent":"Parse OSM results into tidy dataframe","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# Parse into a tidy data frame\nosm_munis <- purrr::map_dfr(osm_result$elements, function(el) {\n  tags <- el$tags\n  tibble(\n    osm_id     = as.character(el$id),\n    name       = tags$name %||% NA_character_,\n    wikidata   = tags$wikidata %||% NA_character_,\n    admin_level = tags$admin_level %||% NA_character_,\n    is_in_state = tags[[\"is_in:state\"]] %||% NA_character_,\n    is_in_province = tags[[\"is_in:province\"]] %||% NA_character_\n  )\n})\n\ncat(\"Total OSM municipal relations:\", nrow(osm_munis), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"With wikidata tag:\", sum(!is.na(osm_munis$wikidata)), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Without wikidata tag:\", sum(is.na(osm_munis$wikidata)), \"\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01EjveNeQVWxhJ9DqcfU5JXv"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"content","value":[{"type":"text","text":"Output:\nTotal OSM municipal relations: 341 \nWith wikidata tag: 338 \nWithout wikidata tag: 3 \n\n\nExecution time: 4857ms"}]},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"Total OSM municipal relations: 341 \nWith wikidata tag: 338 \nWithout wikidata tag: 3"},"elapsedMs":4927}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01PMjM5JmCBpBZMAG4dWt8MC"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# Now join: which Wikidata municipalities are missing from the OSM wikidata tags?\n# And wh...[truncated, total 942 chars]","intent":"Match missing-P402 municipalities to OSM via wikidata tags"},"intent":"Match missing-P402 municipalities to OSM via wikidata tags","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# Now join: which Wikidata municipalities are missing from the OSM wikidata tags?\n# And which OSM relations have QIDs that match our missing-P402 set?\n\nwd_ine <- readRDS(\"data/municipalities_wd_ine.rds\") |> select(qid, ine_code, label_en, label_es)\nwd_p402 <- readRDS(\"data/municipalities_wd_p402.rds\")\n\n# Municipalities missing P402\nmissing_p402 <- wd_p402 |>\n  filter(is.na(osm_relation_id)) |>\n  left_join(wd_ine |> select(qid, ine_code), by = \"qid\")\n\ncat(\"WD municipalities missing P402:\", nrow(missing_p402), \"\\n\\n\")\n\n# Match via wikidata tag on OSM relations\nosm_with_qid <- osm_munis |> filter(!is.na(wikidata))\n\n# Which of our missing-P402 QIDs appear in OSM's wikidata tags?\nmatched_via_tag <- missing_p402 |>\n  inner_join(osm_with_qid |> select(osm_id, wikidata), by = c(\"qid\" = \"wikidata\"))\n\ncat(\"Matched via OSM wikidata tag:\", nrow(matched_via_tag), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Still unmatched:\", nrow(missing_p402) - nrow(matched_via_tag), \"\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01PMjM5JmCBpBZMAG4dWt8MC"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"content","value":[{"type":"text","text":"Output:\nWD municipalities missing P402: 71 \n\nMatched via OSM wikidata tag: 42 \nStill unmat...[truncated, total 123 chars]"}]},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"WD municipalities missing P402: 71 \n\nMatched via OSM wikidata tag: 42 \nStill unmatched: 29"},"elapsedMs":823}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01C9Bd2P4b44jhjqdbVowzZF"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# For the remaining 29, try matching by name\nstill_missing <- missing_p402 |>\n  filter(!qi...[truncated, total 1527 chars]","intent":"Try name-based matching for remaining municipalities"},"intent":"Try name-based matching for remaining municipalities","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# For the remaining 29, try matching by name\nstill_missing <- missing_p402 |>\n  filter(!qid %in% matched_via_tag$qid)\n\n# Clean OSM names for matching: strip \"Municipio de \", \"Municipio \", etc.\nosm_unmatched <- osm_munis |>\n  filter(!wikidata %in% wd_p402$qid | is.na(wikidata)) |>\n  mutate(\n    name_clean = name |>\n      stringr::str_remove(\"^Municipio( de)?\\\\s+\") |>\n      stringr::str_remove(\"^Gobierno Autónomo Municipal( de)?\\\\s+\")\n  )\n\n# Clean WD names\nstill_missing <- still_missing |>\n  mutate(\n    name_es_clean = label_es |>\n      stringr::str_remove(\"^Municipio( de)?\\\\s+\") |>\n      stringr::str_remove(\"\\\\s*\\\\(municipio\\\\)$\"),\n    name_en_clean = label_en |>\n      stringr::str_remove(\"\\\\s+[Mm]unicipality$\")\n  )\n\ncat(\"Still missing from WD:\", nrow(still_missing), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"OSM relations not yet linked:\", nrow(osm_unmatched), \"\\n\\n\")\n\n# Try matching on cleaned names\nmatch_es <- still_missing |>\n  inner_join(osm_unmatched |> select(osm_id, name_clean, is_in_state),\n             by = c(\"name_es_clean\" = \"name_clean\"))\n\nmatch_en <- still_missing |>\n  filter(!qid %in% match_es$qid) |>\n  inner_join(osm_unmatched |> select(osm_id, name_clean, is_in_state),\n             by = c(\"name_en_clean\" = \"name_clean\"))\n\ncat(\"Matched via Spanish name:\", nrow(match_es), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Matched via English name:\", nrow(match_en), \"\\n\")\n\nremaining <- still_missing |>\n  filter(!qid %in% c(match_es$qid, match_en$qid))\ncat(\"Still unmatched:\", nrow(remaining), \"\\n\\n\")\nremaining |> select(qid, label_en, label_es) |> print(n = 30)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01BMi2fF2hofCwRt6oPSN1Fy"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# San Pedro de Tiquina — check if it's in the free list with a different name\nosm_still_fr...[truncated, total 621 chars]","intent":"Search OSM for San Pedro de Tiquina"},"intent":"Search OSM for San Pedro de Tiquina","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# San Pedro de Tiquina — check if it's in the free list with a different name\nosm_still_free |> filter(grepl(\"Tiquina|Pedro\", name, ignore.case = TRUE))\n# Not there. Let me search OSM directly\nresp <- POST(\n  \"https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter\",\n  body = list(data = '[out:json];relation[\"name\"~\"Tiquina\"][\"boundary\"=\"administrative\"];out tags;'),\n  encode = \"form\"\n)\ntiq <- jsonlite::fromJSON(content(resp, \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"), simplifyVector = FALSE)\ncat(\"Tiquina results:\", length(tiq$elements), \"\\n\")\nif (length(tiq$elements) > 0) {\n  for (el in tiq$elements) cat(\"  \", el$id, \":\", el$tags$name, \"\\n\")\n}","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01F7RfSZVMJyWdhv1upGsDDW"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# San Pedro de Tiquina is also not in OSM. \n# Now let's build the manual matches for the 3...[truncated, total 1032 chars]","intent":"Compile all OSM matches"},"intent":"Compile all OSM matches","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# San Pedro de Tiquina is also not in OSM. \n# Now let's build the manual matches for the 3 the user identified, plus compile everything.\n\nmanual_osm_matches <- tribble(\n  ~qid,        ~osm_id,\n  \"Q591120\",   \"4499870\",   # La Paz = Municipio Nuestra Señora de La Paz\n  \"Q920327\",   \"4507915\",   # Yamparáez = Municipio Yamparaez\n  \"Q1108295\",  \"4511328\",   # Puerto Suarez\n)\n\n# Not in OSM (no relation exists):\nnot_in_osm <- c(\n  \"Q1552084\",  # Tito Yupanqui\n  \"Q1544578\",  # Nazacara de Pacajes\n  \"Q775675\",   # Chuquihuta\n  \"Q1147210\",  # Ckochas\n  \"Q520394\",   # Copacabana\n  \"Q1819398\"   # San Pedro de Tiquina\n)\n\n# Combine all matches: tag-based + name-based + manual\nall_osm_matches <- bind_rows(\n  matched_via_tag |> select(qid, osm_id),\n  match_es |> select(qid, osm_id),\n  manual_osm_matches\n) |> distinct(qid, .keep_all = TRUE)\n\ncat(\"Total matched:\", nrow(all_osm_matches), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Not in OSM:\", length(not_in_osm), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Accounted for:\", nrow(all_osm_matches) + length(not_in_osm), \"of\", nrow(missing_p402), \"\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Let me redo the name matching properly,\n# then look up the correct OSM relations for those 4 separately.\n\n# Redo match_es with NA protection\nmatch_es_fixed <- still_missing |>\n  filter(!is.na(name_es_clean)) |>\n  inner_join(\n    osm_unmatched |> filter(!is.na(name_clean)) |> select(osm_id, name_clean, is_in_state),\n    by = c(\"name_es_clean\" = \"name_clean\")\n  )\n\ncat(\"Fixed Spanish name matches:\", nrow(match_es_fixed), \"\\n\")\n\n# Check: any duplicates now?\nmatch_es_fixed |> count(osm_id, sort = TRUE) |> filter(n > 1)\nmatch_es_fixed |> count(qid, sort = TRUE) |> filter(n > 1)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

The OSM relations have\n# wikidata tags pointing to QIDs that aren't instances of Q1062710.\n# This likely means the OSM wikidata tags are wrong, or these are city\n# QIDs vs municipality QIDs.\n\n# For our purposes, the correct WD items for these municipalities are the ones\n# in our dataset, and the OSM relations exist but point to different QIDs.\n# We should still link P402 from our QIDs to the correct OSM relations.\n\n# But first: San Pedro de Macha. Let me search more specifically.\nresp <- POST(\n  \"https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter\",\n  body = list(data = '[out:json];relation[\"name\"~\"Macha\"][\"boundary\"=\"administrative\"][\"admin_level\"=\"8\"];out tags;'),\n  encode = \"form\"\n)\nmacha <- jsonlite::fromJSON(content(resp, \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"), simplifyVector = FALSE)\ncat(\"Macha results:\", length(macha$elements), \"\\n\")\nfor (el in macha$elements) cat(\"  \", el$id, \":\", el$tags$name, \"(wikidata:\", el$tags$wikidata %||% \"none\", \")\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Add it to the not_in_osm list.\n# \n# Now build the manual matches for the 4 that DO exist but with different QIDs on OSM:\nmanual_osm_matches_extra <- tribble(\n  ~qid,          ~osm_id,\n  \"Q624485\",     \"4126929\",   # Uncía Municipality\n  \"Q647748\",     \"4494694\",   # Caracollo Municipality\n  \"Q1153550\",    \"4511510\",   # Mineros Municipality\n)\n\nnot_in_osm_final <- c(\n  \"Q1552084\",    # Tito Yupanqui\n  \"Q1544578\",    # Nazacara de Pacajes\n  \"Q775675\",     # Chuquihuta\n  \"Q1147210\",    # Ckochas\n  \"Q520394\",     # Copacabana\n  \"Q1819398\",    # San Pedro de Tiquina\n  \"Q106409795\"   # San Pedro de Macha\n)\n\n# Rebuild all matches cleanly\nall_osm_matches <- bind_rows(\n  matched_via_tag |> select(qid, osm_id),\n  match_es_fixed |> select(qid, osm_id),\n  manual_osm_matches,\n  manual_osm_matches_extra\n) |> distinct(qid, .keep_all = TRUE)\n\n# Verify: no duplicate osm_ids\ndupes <- all_osm_matches |> count(osm_id, sort = TRUE) |> filter(n > 1)\ncat(\"Duplicate osm_id assignments:\", nrow(dupes), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Total matched:\", nrow(all_osm_matches), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Not in OSM:\", length(not_in_osm_final), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Accounted for:\", nrow(all_osm_matches) + length(not_in_osm_final), \"of\", nrow(missing_p402), \"\\n\")","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_016kbJ9KTyeET2BCrbGArVBe"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"write","input":{"file_path":"add_osm_relations_to_wikidata.R","content":"# add_osm_relations_to_wikidata.R\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────...[truncated, total 5648 chars]"},"caption":"add_osm_relations_to_wikidata.R","captionLink":"add_osm_relations_to_wikidata.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# add_osm_relations_to_wikidata.R\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Finds OSM boundary relations for Bolivian municipalities missing Wikidata P402\n# (OpenStreetMap relation ID) and generates QuickStatements to add them.\n#\n# Matching strategy:\n#   1. Query all admin_level=8 boundary relations in Bolivia from Overpass\n#   2. Match via `wikidata` tag on OSM relations (most reliable)\n#   3. Match by cleaned municipality name (Spanish label vs OSM name)\n#   4. Manual matches for remaining discrepancies\n#\n# Prerequisites:\n#   - data/municipalities_wd_p402.rds  (from get_wikidata_instances with P402)\n#   - data/municipalities_wd_ine.rds   (QID ↔ INE crosswalk)\n#   - R/create_quick_statement.R\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(httr)\nlibrary(jsonlite)\nlibrary(stringr)\n\nsource(\"R/create_quick_statement.R\")\n\n# ── 1. Load Wikidata municipality data ────────────────────────────────────────\n\nwd_ine <- readRDS(\"data/municipalities_wd_ine.rds\") |>\n  select(qid, ine_code, label_en, label_es)\n\nwd_p402 <- readRDS(\"data/municipalities_wd_p402.rds\")\n\nmissing_p402 <- wd_p402 |>\n  filter(is.na(osm_relation_id)) |>\n  left_join(wd_ine |> select(qid, ine_code, label_es), by = \"qid\")\n\ncat(\"Municipalities missing P402:\", nrow(missing_p402), \"\\n\")\n\n# ── 2. Query OSM for all Bolivian municipal boundary relations ────────────────\n\noverpass_query <- '\n[out:json][timeout:180];\narea[\"ISO3166-1\"=\"BO\"]->.bolivia;\nrelation[\"boundary\"=\"administrative\"][\"admin_level\"=\"8\"](area.bolivia);\nout tags;\n'\n\nresp <- POST(\n  \"https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter\",\n  body = list(data = overpass_query),\n  encode = \"form\"\n)\n\nosm_result <- fromJSON(content(resp, \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"),\n                       simplifyVector = FALSE)\n\nosm_munis <- purrr::map_dfr(osm_result$elements, function(el) {\n  tags <- el$tags\n  tibble(\n    osm_id         = as.character(el$id),\n    name           = tags$name %||% NA_character_,\n    wikidata       = tags$wikidata %||% NA_character_,\n    admin_level    = tags$admin_level %||% NA_character_,\n    is_in_state    = tags[[\"is_in:state\"]] %||% NA_character_,\n    is_in_province = tags[[\"is_in:province\"]] %||% NA_character_\n  )\n})\n\ncat(\"OSM admin_level=8 relations found:\", nrow(osm_munis), \"\\n\")\n\n# ── 3. Round 1: Match via wikidata tag on OSM relations ───────────────────────\n\nosm_with_qid <- osm_munis |> filter(!is.na(wikidata))\n\nmatched_via_tag <- missing_p402 |>\n  inner_join(osm_with_qid |> select(osm_id, wikidata), by = c(\"qid\" = \"wikidata\"))\n\ncat(\"Round 1 (wikidata tag):\", nrow(matched_via_tag), \"matched\\n\")\n\n# ── 4. Round 2: Match by cleaned Spanish name ────────────────────────────────\n\nstill_missing <- missing_p402 |>\n  filter(!qid %in% matched_via_tag$qid) |>\n  mutate(\n    name_es_clean = label_es |>\n      str_remove(\"^Municipio( de)?\\\\s+\") |>\n      str_remove(\"\\\\s*\\\\(municipio\\\\)$\")\n  )\n\nosm_unmatched <- osm_munis |>\n  filter(!wikidata %in% wd_p402$qid | is.na(wikidata)) |>\n  mutate(\n    name_clean = name |>\n      str_remove(\"^Municipio( de)?\\\\s+\") |>\n      str_remove(\"^Gobierno Aut\\u00f3nomo Municipal( de)?\\\\s+\")\n  )\n\nmatched_by_name <- still_missing |>\n  filter(!is.na(name_es_clean)) |>\n  inner_join(\n    osm_unmatched |> filter(!is.na(name_clean)) |> select(osm_id, name_clean),\n    by = c(\"name_es_clean\" = \"name_clean\")\n  )\n\ncat(\"Round 2 (name match):\", nrow(matched_by_name), \"matched\\n\")\n\n# ── 5. Round 3: Manual matches ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Names that diverge between Wikidata and OSM, or OSM relation has a different\n# QID in its wikidata tag (typically a city QID vs municipality QID).\n\nmanual_osm_matches <- tribble(\n  ~qid,          ~osm_id,\n  \"Q591120\",     \"4499870\",   # La Paz = Municipio Nuestra Señora de La Paz\n  \"Q920327\",     \"4507915\",   # Yamparáez = Municipio Yamparaez\n  \"Q1108295\",    \"4511328\",   # Puerto Suárez\n  \"Q624485\",     \"4126929\",   # Uncía Municipality (OSM tags Q1642052)\n  \"Q647748\",     \"4494694\",   # Caracollo Municipality (OSM tags Q647907)\n  \"Q1153550\",    \"4511510\",   # Mineros Municipality (OSM tags Q602690)\n)\n\n# Municipalities confirmed absent from OSM (no boundary relation exists):\nnot_in_osm <- c(\n  \"Q1552084\",    # Tito Yupanqui\n  \"Q1544578\",    # Nazacara de Pacajes\n  \"Q775675\",     # Chuquihuta\n  \"Q1147210\",    # Ckochas\n  \"Q520394\",     # Copacabana\n  \"Q1819398\",    # San Pedro de Tiquina\n  \"Q106409795\"   # San Pedro de Macha\n)\n\n# ── 6. Combine and validate ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nall_osm_matches <- bind_rows(\n  matched_via_tag |> select(qid, osm_id),\n  matched_by_name |> select(qid, osm_id),\n  manual_osm_matches\n) |> distinct(qid, .keep_all = TRUE)\n\n# Sanity checks\nstopifnot(\"Duplicate osm_id assignments\" =\n            nrow(all_osm_matches |> count(osm_id) |> filter(n > 1)) == 0)\nstopifnot(\"All municipalities accounted for\" =\n            nrow(all_osm_matches) + length(not_in_osm) == nrow(missing_p402))\n\ncat(\"\\nTotal matched:\", nrow(all_osm_matches), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"Not in OSM:\", length(not_in_osm), \"\\n\")\n\n# ── 7. Generate QuickStatements ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\np402_statements <- all_osm_matches |>\n  left_join(wd_ine |> select(qid, label_en, ine_code), by = \"qid\") |>\n  add_quick_statement_column(qid, \"P402\", osm_id)\n\ncat(\"\\n\", nrow(p402_statements), \"QuickStatements generated.\\n\")\ncat(\"Preview:\\n\")\nhead(p402_statements |> select(label_en, qid, osm_id), 5)\n\n# Copy to clipboard or write to file:\n# writeLines(p402_statements$quick_statement)\n# writeLines(p402_statements$quick_statement, \"output/p402_quickstatements.txt\")\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01AQx26DVF7vde2Ms6PriAXG"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# I defined not_in_osm_final from not_in_osm (6 items), but Q106409795 was in\n# not_in_osm...[truncated, total 637 chars]","intent":"Fix accounting to include all 7 not-in-OSM"},"intent":"Fix accounting to include all 7 not-in-OSM","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# I defined not_in_osm_final from not_in_osm (6 items), but Q106409795 was in\n# not_in_osm (7 items) defined earlier. <div><tool-call id="toolu_01HXQpVak64DPsX73PpoKxhr"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"# adm3_pcode = \"BO\" + 6-digit INE code\n# Extract 6-digit INE code and filter to our 7 miss...[truncated, total 959 chars]","intent":"Extract the 7 missing municipalities from the shapefile"},"intent":"Extract the 7 missing municipalities from the shapefile","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# adm3_pcode = \"BO\" + 6-digit INE code\n# Extract 6-digit INE code and filter to our 7 missing municipalities\n\nnot_in_osm_df <- tribble(\n  ~qid,          ~ine_code, ~label_en,\n  \"Q1552084\",    \"021703\",  \"Tito Yupanqui Municipality\",\n  \"Q1544578\",    \"020307\",  \"Nazacara de Pacajes Municipality\",\n  \"Q775675\",     \"050204\",  \"Chuquihuta Municipality\",\n  \"Q1147210\",    \"051103\",  \"Ckochas\",\n  \"Q520394\",     \"021701\",  \"Copacabana Municipality\",\n  \"Q1819398\",    \"021702\",  \"San Pedro de Tiquina Municipality\",\n  \"Q106409795\",  \"050405\",  \"San Pedro de Macha Municipality\"\n)\n\nmissing_shapes <- admin3 |>\n  mutate(ine_code = substr(adm3_pcode, 3, 8)) |>\n  inner_join(not_in_osm_df, by = \"ine_code\") |>\n  select(qid, ine_code, label_en, adm3_name, adm1_name, adm2_name,\n         adm3_pcode, geometry)\n\ncat(\"Found:\", nrow(missing_shapes), \"of 7 expected municipalities\\n\")\nmissing_shapes |> st_drop_geometry() |> select(ine_code, adm3_name, adm1_name, adm2_name)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>
